### Page 2

2  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/
上週重要市場資訊
⚫ 美國聯準會如市場預期 ，維持政策利率不變。 決策官員仍對通膨保持擔憂 ， 也承
認美國經濟正以「強勁速度」成長。聯準會主席Kevin Warsh在會後談話中，並
未明確排除進一步緊縮政策的可能，激勵市場對12 月升息的預期升溫。數據方
面，美國第三季 GDP 年化成長率達 3.4%，高於前值的 2.2%，亦優於市場預期
的 3.2%，印證美國經濟動能強勁。此外，9 月 PCE 與核心 PCE 符合預期，但凸
顯通膨壓力持續偏高。在 「通膨高企」 與 「經濟強韌」 雙重因素驅動下，市場預
期聯準會將於 12 月升息，為美元指數提供有力支撐。惟近期受 AI 產業股票波
動加劇影響，部分資金轉入美債避險，導致美元指數在高檔面臨調節性賣壓。
⚫ 儘管受能源價格走揚影響，9 月歐元區通膨率升至 3.8%，但在薪資壓力維持溫
和，且歐洲央行尚未觀察到第二輪通膨效應全面擴散的情況下 ，歐洲央行上週決
議維持政策利率不變 。通膨回升令市場預期歐洲央行將在下次會議升息 ， 為歐元

提供部分支撐 ， 但考量歐元區經濟成長表現仍落後美國 ， 在美元基本面強勁的抑
制下 ， 歐元反彈力道相對有限 。 目前歐元最大的潛在風險在於部分國家的債券市
場壓力尚未消散，市場對歐洲財政風險仍保持高度警戒。
⚫ 日本央行維持政策利率於 1.25%不變，並且首次明確表示已達成其 2%的通膨目
標；過去日本央行雖認為通膨改善 ， 但始終強調尚未穩定達標，如今首次正式承
認達成 2%目標，代表未來升息門檻進一步下降。此外，日本央行將經濟成長預
測維持在 0.7%至 0.8%，並小幅上修 CPI 預測，令市場預期日央 12 月有望再次
升息，日本貨幣政策正常化將有助日圓結束疲軟走勢。

### Page 3

3  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
 美元兌新台幣匯率走勢  
Nov/2/2026 
＜上週走勢＞ 
    上週台幣匯價高點為 32.390，低點在 32.676，以 32.478 作收，較前一周收盤價
升值 3 分。 
上週台幣先升後貶，周初，市場等待聯準會利率決議，交投偏謹慎觀望，而外
資在前周大幅匯出之後，轉為雙向進出，台幣止跌回穩，最高來到 32.390 的周內高
點；然而周間，美國科技股財報表現失色，台股賣壓沉重，外資賣超台股並再度轉
為大幅匯出，台幣走貶，最低來到 32.676，不過匯價逢高吸引出口商賣壓出籠，加
上央行提供流動性，台幣貶幅收斂，終場在央行調節之下收在 32.478。

＜未來分析＞ 
    預估本週台幣偏弱震盪。就國際市場來看，聯準會 10 月利率決策維持利率不
變，但有兩名官員主張升息 1 碼，且聯準會表示通膨仍處於高檔，顯示聯準會對通
膨仍保持警戒，市場預期美國利率將維持高檔，台美利差仍維持在相對高位，不利
台幣表現；另一方面，美國科技股財報表現不如預期，引發市場對AI 投資回報的疑
慮，對台股也帶來壓力。就台灣市場來看，外資持續調節台股並大幅匯出，10 月累
計賣超將近兆元，資金匯出壓力大，不過一方面央行持續進場調節，加上匯價逢高
預計吸引出口商進場拋匯，料將限制台幣貶值速度。綜合以上，預估本週台幣走勢
偏弱。 
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌台幣：32.300-32.800

### Page 4

4  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌人民幣匯率走勢  
Nov/2/2026 
＜上週走勢＞ 
上週人民幣持續走升。周初市場等待美國聯準會利率決議，交投偏向觀望，境
外人民幣主要於 6.6600 上方震盪； 周間聯準會如預期維持利率不變 ，美元指數自 102
上方回落 ， 加上中國出口結匯需求持續提供支撐， 境外人民幣突破6.6600 整數關口；
週五人行中間價續創近三年九個月新高，最高來到 6.7138，顯示中國人行允許人民
幣溫和升值的走勢，境外人民幣一度升破 6.6500 整數關口，最高來到 6.6487，終場
收在 6.6528，週線升值 162 點，境內人民幣則收在 6.6558，週線升值 146 點。

＜未來分析＞ 
     預估本週人民幣偏升 。 就中國國內市場來看 ，10 月官方製造業 PMI 降至 49.3，
重新落入景氣收縮區間，反映中國內需及製造業動能仍有待改善，不過 9 月出口年
增 36.3%，出口商結匯需求持續提供人民幣支撐，加上人行中間價續創近三年九個
月新高，預計將持續帶動人民幣走升；就國際市場來看，聯準會 10 月利率決策維持
利率不變，但有兩名官員主張升息 1 碼，顯示聯準會對通膨仍保持警戒，市場預期
美國利率將維持高檔 ， 美中利差維持在相對高位 ，將限制人民幣升值的速度與幅度 ；
最後就技術面來看 ，USD/CNH 日線圖中，各天期均線向下發散 ，匯價維持在五日均
線之下交投，均線呈現空頭排列，顯示空方勢力較盛，綜合以上，預估本週人民幣
偏升。

＜預估本週＞ 美元兌人民幣：6.6200-6.6700

### Page 5

5  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌日圓匯率走勢  
         Nov/02/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週 USD/JPY 上漲。週初，美伊衝突仍陷僵局支撐美元避險買盤，USD/JPY 最
高來到 158.34；週間，聯準會公布利率維持利率不變，USD/JPY 持續在 158 附近盤
整；臨近週末，日本央行公布利率維持不變並釋放偏鷹訊號， USD/JPY 最低來到
156.94，終場收在 157.53，週線上漲 72 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 USD/JPY 區間震盪，主要原因如下：一、日本央行於上周五宣布利
率維持在 1.25%不變，並表示隨著基礎通膨率大致達到 2%，央行必須持續控制通
膨穩定於此目標，且再次強調貨幣政策已進入更為緊縮階段，未來每隔數月即可能
升息。此外，日本央行在季度報告中，亦將截至 2028 年 3 月財年的核心消費者通
膨預估自三個月前預測的 2.4%上調至 2.6%。日本央行偏鷹立場令市場預期日本最
快將於 12 月再次升息，預計將有利日圓表現。二、聯準會宣布維持利率於 3.75%-
4%區間不變，惟因通膨仍處高位，聯準會重申將致力實現 2%的長期通膨區間， 
FedWatch 顯示 12 月升息機率約七成，美元持穩，預計將限縮 USD/JPY 下行空
間。三、就技術面而言，在日線圖中，USD/JPY 各天期均線趨平，近期匯價大致
在 156 至 158 間震盪，匯價暫無明確方向性。綜合以上，預估 USD/JPY 本週呈現
區間震盪的可能性較高。 
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌日圓：156.00-158.50 
前週交易區間 
156.63 158.34

### Page 6

6  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌歐元匯率走勢  
             Nov/02/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
  上週 EUR/USD 下跌。週初，美伊衝突持續以及法國政經情勢動盪等情況下，
歐元走跌，最低來到 1.1111；週間，聯準會和歐洲央行相繼公布利率決議均維持不
變，歐洲央行發表言論偏鷹派，歐元止跌回升至 1.1214；臨近週末，歐元區公布經
濟數據好壞參半，歐元自高點回落，終場收在 1.1158，週線下跌 41 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 EUR/USD 區間震盪，主要原因如下：一、歐洲央行如市場預期宣布
存款利率維持在 2.5%不變，並警告能源成本持續上漲將對通膨造成上行風險，暗示
可能在 12 月再次升息 ， 升息預期升溫預計將支撐歐元走勢 。二 、 歐元區第三季GDP
年增 1.3%， 高於市場預期的1.1%， 顯示在能源價格持續上漲的不利因素下 ，經濟仍
展現韌性，預計有利歐元表現。三、聯準會宣布維持利率在 3.75%-4%不變，並重申
致力控制通膨的立場 。 此外 ，美國公布第三季 GDP 成長 3.4%， 優於市場預期的3.2%，
9 月核心 PCE 年增率自前月的 3%上升至 3.1%，在美國經濟強勁及通膨仍高的情況
下 ， 市場預期聯準會將於 12 月升息一碼，美元持穩 ，預計將限縮歐元走升空間 。四、
技術面而言，在日線圖中，EUR/USD 各天期均線向下發散，不過近期匯價在多次向
下測試 1.1120 附近均未有效跌破 ， 顯示下方仍有買盤支撐。綜合以上 ， 預估EUR/USD
本週呈現區間震盪的可能性較高。 
＜預估本週＞ 歐元兌美元：1.1050-1.1270 
前週交易區間 
1.1103 1.1226

7  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
                           新台幣利率走勢分析

115.10.27-115.10.30 
銀行隔夜拆款利率 0.805% ~ 0.830%  
30 天期短票利率   1.66% ~ 1.68% 
公債－10 年期-A15109R 2.03% 
－ 5 年期- A15108 1.845%~1.846%

一、 台幣貨幣市場部分 ， 時序邁入月底 ， 銀行間已大致調整好積數 ， 且央行未明顯
增發定存單，令市場資金充裕。近兩日股市回落，令週五資金需求上揚，惟供
需尚屬平衡，因此短率持穩於近期區間。 
週 五 隔 夜 拆 款 利 率 區 間 在 0.805%~0.825% ， 30 天 期 短 票 主 要 成 交 在
1.66%~1.68%，利率大致持平於上週。 
二、 本週台債市場交投依舊清淡 ，10 年期指標券 A15109R 等殖系統成交在 2.03%，
5 年期指標券 A15108 議價成交在 1.845%~1.846%。 
美債利率自波段高點回落，且央行總裁楊金龍本週立法院發言維持近來對通
膨容忍度變寬之態度 ， 惟市場對央行升息預期並未完全消除 ， 買盤追價動力有
限，預期台債殖利率維持區間整理，關注下週30 年期公債標售果，預估 5 年
券區間為 1.80%~1.85%，10 年券區間為 1.99%~2.04%。

市場焦點
美國經濟
• 第三季GDP初值季增年率為 3.4%，高於市場估計的 3.2%(前季GDP為2.2%)。美國9月個人消
費支出(PCE)物價指數月增0.5%，年增3.6%；核心PCE指數月增0.3%、年增3.1%。
• 美國新一輪總體經濟數據偏向支撐股市宏觀多頭格局 ，然而中東衝突持續陷入僵局 、市場對
於AI投資前景的疑慮持續 ，可能加大短期股市波動風險 。考慮到當前市場風險與機會並存 ，
持股建議聚焦大型藍籌股，並適度多元分散產業布局。
期中選舉
• 美國期中選舉將於11月3日登場，若民主黨成功奪回眾議院，並進一步取得參議院多數席次 ，
將提高川普後續推動政策的難度 。觀察過去美國總統四年任期的選舉週期 ，期中選舉的隔年
通常是股市表現最佳的年度 ，其背後可能反映期中選舉落幕後 ，政治不確定性降低 ，市場對

政策方向的預期逐漸明朗，且政府可能透過財政或經濟政策支撐成長。
中國
• 9月工業企業利潤年成長 14.4%，增速較8月的4.2%明顯加快 ，中國工業利潤改善 ，主要由AI
硬體與能源價格上漲帶動 ，其中IT設備和有色金屬為AI的兩大受益行業，化學製品企業則是能
源價格上漲的最大受益者之一 ，短期而言AI、高技術製造與能源產業仍是市場主線 ，但內需疲
弱與價格競爭問題未解，投資上仍應以大型龍頭企業為主。

美國第三季GDP成長超預期、通膨壓力續增
• 美國第三季經濟成長率(GDP)創一年來最大增幅：美國經濟分析局(BEA)公布，第三季GDP初值季增年率為3.4%，高於市場
估計的3.2%(前季GDP為2.2%)。企業AI投資火熱、消費者開支保持韌性，抵銷了油價上漲衝擊。
• 9月數據顯示通膨壓力續增 ，但符合預期：美國9月個人消費支出(PCE)物價指數月增0.5%，寫3月以來最大月增幅；年增幅
3.6%。剔除食物與能源的9月核心PCE指數，月增0.3%、年增3.1%。
• 市場反應：由於新一輪通膨數據符合預期 ，市場未改變聯準會 12月利率決議升息一碼的預測 。然而，有跡象顯示實質個人
消費支出開始轉弱，儲蓄率也有所下降，市場開始押注通膨可能已經見頂。從聯邦利率期貨市場數據來看，市場對於明年升

息空間的預期已較上個月收斂(九月底預測升息2~3碼→目前預測1~2碼)，
• 觀點：美國新一輪總體經濟數據偏向支撐股市宏觀多頭格局，然而中東衝突持續陷入僵局、市場對於AI投資前景的疑慮持續，
可能加大短期股市波動風險。考慮到當前市場風險與機會並存，持股建議聚焦大型藍籌股，並適度多元分散產業布局。
資料來源：Bloomberg，兆豐銀行整理，2026.10.30。

聯準會貨幣決議與聲明稿差異
資料來源：Federal Reserve（10/28、9/16 聲明）；鉅亨網，2026/10/29。以上為聲明重點整理。
2 / 5
10月決議｜按兵不動
3.75% – 4.00%
10票贊成、2票反對
經濟措辭轉強 「穩健擴張」改為「強勁擴張」；國內支出具韌性， 生產力與資本投資表現強。
通膨仍未解除 聲明維持「通膨居高」的判斷，未再宣稱本次升息有 助加快回到2%目標。
政策訊號更具彈性 改稱需要更多資訊判斷適當政策立場，未承諾下一步 升息或降息。
關鍵變化
理事庫克(Lisa D. Cook) 和克里夫蘭聯準銀行總裁哈瑪克(Beth M. Hammack)主張本次升息1碼，
形成2張鷹派反對票；9月決議則為一致通過。利率不變不等於轉向寬鬆，委員會內部對通膨風險仍

有不同判斷。
市場解讀：本次是「暫停觀察」；12月是否再升息，仍取決於通膨、需求與能源價格。

聯準會會後記者會問答重點整理
資料來源：Federal Reserve 10/28會後記者會；Reuters、Investing.com，2026/10/28。
3 / 5
01｜市場利率反映緊縮壓力
華許指出，9月與 10月兩次 會議之
間，美國公債名目與實質殖利率明
顯走高；市場利率可先行反映成長
、通膨與風險變化，不必等政策利
率調整。
政策含意
長天期殖利率仍受通膨預期、期限
溢酬及公債供需影響；政策利率不
變，不代表債市壓力解除。
02｜AI投資與生產力
華許認為美國經濟尚未過熱，AI投
資推升需求，但生產力也有改善；
聯準會將持續評估新科技對產出、
就業與物價的中長期影響。
政策含意
AI支出可支撐企業成長，也伴隨資本
支出回收與融資風險；應檢視現金流
、投資報酬及生產力是否實際提升。

03｜政策制度與工具再檢視
聯準會規劃2027年安排1次、2028
年安排2次專注貨幣政策策略的會
議，不進行利率表決或發布政策聲
明；另將檢視資產負債表政策。
政策含意
制度調整不等於政策轉向寬鬆；仍須
觀察準備金、資產負債表操作及經濟
數據，評估金融條件如何變化。
解讀：華許除談利率外，也聚焦市場利率、AI生產力及政策制度調整；投資判斷仍應回到數據、企業獲利與金融條件。

聯準會利率決議摘要暨投資策略建議
資料來源：Federal Reserve；Reuters、InvestingLive；鉅亨網，2026/10/29。市場價格可能隨後續交易更新。
4 / 5
政策決議 利率維持 3.75% – 4.00% 經濟強、通膨高；政策下一步保留彈性
美股
短線受利率預期與科技股評價牽動
；AI投資支撐獲利，但仍需檢視現
金流與資本支出回報。
美債
若升息預期升溫，短天期殖利率較
敏感；長天期亦受通膨預期、發債
供給與期限溢酬影響。
美元／黃金
美元可能隨利差預期波動；黃金同
時受實質利率、美元及地緣政治避
險需求影響。
投資策略建議｜情境式配置
股票 保留核心持股，避免追價；優先關注獲利能見度、現金流與定價能力較佳企業。
債券 以分散到期日與信用風險為主；可分批配置高品質債券，避免單押長天期利率下行。

多元資產 保留流動性與再平衡空間；若通膨再升、殖利率上行，分批進場並控管整體波動。
後續觀察：美國通膨與就業數據、能源價格、企業財報，以及市場對12月政策的重新定價。

美國期中選舉登場，國會席次變化牽動川普施政
資料來源： 各媒體，2002~2022年
歷屆期中選舉確認結果時間
選舉日期 眾議院控制 權確認時間 參議院控制權 確認時間 時長
2006年11月7日 選舉日當晚 11月9日 2天

2022年11月8日 11月16日 11月12日 8天
• 美國期中選舉登場，國會席次變化牽動川普施政：美國期中選舉將於11月3日登場，為川普第二任期上任以來首場全國性選
舉，除國會議員改選外，亦包括州長及其他公職人員選舉。若民主黨成功奪回眾議院，並進一步取得參議院多數席次，將提
高川普後續推動政策的難度。不過，川普仍可透過發布行政命令等方式，在一定程度上繞過國會的立法制衡，持續推動其政

策議程。
• 本次選舉結果可能耗費一週才能明朗：回顧2006至2022年近五屆美國期中選舉，其中四屆的參眾兩院控制權於投票當晚或
隔日即大致明朗，僅2022年因關鍵席次競爭激烈、郵寄選票計票耗時較長，直至投票後第8天才確認兩院席次歸屬。考量本
次參議院選情膠著，加上川普執政以來美國政治極化持續加劇，不排除本次選舉結果需耗費一週才能明朗，期間市場也可能
面臨額外的不確定性。

期中選舉的隔年通常是股市表現最佳的年度
• 期中選舉的隔年通常是股市表現最佳的年度：觀察過去美國總統四年任期的選舉週期，期中選舉的隔年通常是股市表現最佳
的年度。根據1940至2025年統計，標普500指數第三年平均報酬率達16.5%，高於第一年的8.3%、第二年的4.2%及第四年
的6.9%；中位數報酬率更達18.9%。其背後可能反映期中選舉落幕後，政治不確定性降低，市場對政策方向的預期逐漸明朗，
且政府可能透過財政或經濟政策支撐成長。
• 政黨與國會組成影響有限，基本面仍是關鍵： 進一步觀察政黨差異，1940年以來，第三年由共和黨總統執政時，標普500指
數平均報酬率約18.2%，高於民主黨總統執政時的14.9%，但兩者均呈現正報酬，顯示總統所屬政黨並非決定股市表現的唯

一因素。國會組成亦可能影響政策推動與市場預期，例如共和黨總統搭配民主黨國會時，第三年平均報酬率約15.6%；若國
會呈現混合執政，平均報酬率則達23.1%。

顧
能源與AI價格推動中國工業利潤增速回升
• 能源與AI價格推動中國工業利潤增速回升：9月工業企業利潤年成長14.4%，增速較8月的4.2%明顯加快。但前三季度累計增速
從1到8月的15.7%小幅放緩至15.5%，利潤改善主要反映了能源和 AI硬體價格的上漲，其中AI硬體價格仍是高技術製造業利潤
的重要支撐。前三季度，高技術製造業利潤年成長52.0%，與1到8月54.7%的增速大體相當。拉動整體利潤成長約10個百分點，
貢獻率約三分之二。值得注意的是，利潤成長仍主要集中於少數相關行業 ，其他企業部門整體依然處境艱難 ，內需疲弱疊加價
格競爭激烈令利潤受到擠壓。
• 投資建議：中國工業利潤改善 ，主要由AI硬體與能源價格上漲 帶動，其中IT設備和有色金屬為AI的兩大受益行業，化學製品企

業則是能源價格上漲的最大受益者之一 ，短期而言AI、高技術製造與能源產業仍是市場主線 ，但內需疲弱與價格競爭問題未解 ，
投資上仍應以大型龍頭企業為主。
資料來源：彭博，2026.10.27
能源、AI價格飆升提振中國相關行業利潤增速

中國科技公司享受負殖利率差
• 中國科技公司享受負殖利率差 ：中國在經濟成長放緩與寬鬆貨幣
政策下，借貸成本持續下降 ，10年期公債殖利率由年 初1.86%降
至1.63%，使企業融資條件更加寬鬆 ，相較之下 ，美國受經濟韌
性與高利率政策影響，10年期公債殖利率升至約5%，企業整體融
資成本明顯提高 。此外2025年中國大型科技企業在境內債券市場
融資規模有限 ，僅為約74億美元，而且支付的殖利率 ，平均比同
風 險 層 級 的 債 券 高 出18 個 基 點，但現在 溢 價 已 經 變 為 折 價，
2026年迄今，中國科技企業已在境內 發債約100億美元，新發科
技債殖利率平均折價6個基點，顯示機構投資者信心發生了顯著轉
變，大幅降低了行業推進 AI建設的資金成本 ，會有上述現象是 由

於中國經濟通縮，難以承受更高利率 ，因此官方須維持較寬鬆的
金融條件 ，科技企業剛剛開始通過債券市場融資 ，預料債券將受
到投資者關注。
• 展望後市： 低成本債券融資對中國科技行業構成利好 ，與IPO 不
同，債券市場為企業提供了一個可重複利用的融資渠道 ，在股市
劇烈波動之際 ，對投資人是另一種資產配置 ，可優先關注具資金
優勢與應用場景的大型科技龍頭 ，以及受惠於 AI算力需求成長的
伺服器、網通設備和資料中心產業。

AI產業鏈融資警訊：資金缺口正在擴散
• 資本支出與現金流的嚴重失衡：AI生態系中的巨額投資正考驗著企業，一旦企業營運現金流不足以支持基礎設施建設，就必
須高度依賴外部融資(發債或增資)，使其更容易受到資本市場波動與利率變化的衝擊 。AI算力提供商資金缺口最為顯著 ，其
資本支出已接近營運現金流3倍(284%)，需要投入大額資金採購高端GPU並對外出租算力，數據中心資本支出佔現金流的比
例已從2022年11月(ChatGPT問世)1倍(104%)快速爬升至1.3倍(128%)，超大規模雲服務商資本支出占現金流比例已從2022
年底40%翻倍飆升至73%。在彭博研究統計的8家超大型雲業者中，出現自由現金流為負(資金缺口)公司數量已從2022年的
1家增加至4家(達50%)。

• 產業鏈錯綜複雜，風險具連鎖效應： 從上游晶片製造商 、硬體開發商、建築商、電力/公用事業，到中游的數據中心與雲端
服務商，再到下游的軟體與數據管理公司，其銷售與支付合約彼此高度緊密相連。當算力提供商或數據中心出現資金吃緊時，
風險會迅速向上游傳遞，導致項目延期、融資成本攀升，甚至延後整個生態系的收入實現時程。目前除了雲端與數據中心外，
硬體開發商與建築商中出現資金缺口的企業比例也在上升。
資金缺口正蔓延至人工智能產業鏈一半的層級
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.10.28
算力提供商和數據中心的資本支出超過現金流

市場定價轉變：借貸成本上升與股票估值修正
• 資本市場已經開始針對資金缺口進行風險定價：在資金缺口最大的AI算力提供商、數據中心以及超大規模雲服務商中，其發
行的公司債相較於美國國債的殖利率差 (利差)顯著拓寬，高於一般投資級債券或高收益債券的表現 ，顯示債券市場對其違約
與融資風險要求更高的風險補償。由於大量現金流被基礎設施建設掏空，且AI應用端的獲利回收速度未達市場預期，投資人
不再願意給予極高溢價，整體AI產業鏈的預期本益比出現明顯修正。
• 小結與投資建議：當前AI產業正在經歷基礎設施過渡期，資本密集的算力出租、數據中心建設正遭受強烈的資金擠壓，若未
來1~2年內下游應用端的商業化營收無法順利銜接，依賴高度槓桿與外部融資的企業將面臨嚴峻的流動性挑戰 。對AI算力出

租商及部分高負債的數據中心開發商保持謹慎態度，一旦信貸市場收緊或借貸成本升高，利潤與營運將受重創。晶片製造商
的經營現金流持續成長，財務結構改善，選擇擁有強大訂價權與現金流護城河的上游半導體巨頭，安全邊際最高，電力與公
用事業資本支出佔現金流比例相對穩定且受惠於剛性需求。超大規模雲服務商中表現差異大，優先選擇資產負債表健全、資
產回報率高且擁有傳統核心業務能持續提供穩定現金流的科技巨頭，遠離需大量靠發債維持資本支出的個股。
超大規模雲服務商和數據中心的利差擴大
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.10.28
隨著借貸成本波動加劇，AI股權估值走弱

顧 • 利率可能維持高檔更久，但收益價值正在浮現：近月以
來美國 10年期公債殖利率一度升抵 5.36%，30年期接
近5.7%，反映市場重新評價未來利率中樞 、財政赤字
及資金需求等因素。雖然長天期債券價格仍可能受到利
率波動影響，但高殖利率已大幅提高新進場資金的收益
起點，投資焦點應由預測利率轉向評估收益價值。
• 債券標售與資金流顯示配置需求逐步回溫： 10月美國
財政部10年期、20年期及30年期公債標售需求維持穩
健，投標倍數約介於2.5至2.8倍區間；同時，美國債券
基金已連續 26週呈現資金淨流入 ，顯示市場未必認為
利率已見頂 ，但已開始接受 5%以上殖利率的長期配置
價值。
• 收益優先取代押注利率轉折： 在Higher for Longer環
境下，債券的核心功能重新回到穩定收益、分散風險與

降低組合波動 。建議可以 3至7年期美國公債及高評等
投資級公司債作為核心收益部位，長天期債券則採分批
布局方式，兼顧收益來源及未來利率循環變化帶來的潛
在機會。

日本

株式会社大和総研 丸の内オフィス 〒100-6756 東京都千代田区丸の内一丁目 9番 1号 グラントウキョウ ノースタワー

2026年 10月 30日 全7頁

2026年 7-9月期 GDP（1次速報）予測 
～前期比年率＋1.5％を予想～ 
個人消費は小幅減だった一方、設備投資と輸出が実質GDPを押し上げ

経済調査部 エコノミスト 畑中 宏仁 
  チーフエコノミスト 神田 慶司

[要約] 
◼ 11月 16日公表予定の 2026年 7-9月期の GDP1次速報では、実質 GDPが前期比年率＋
1.5％（前期比＋0.4％）と、4四半期連続のプラス成長を予想する。個人消費は小幅
に減少したが、設備投資と輸出の増加が実質GDPを押し上げたようだ。 
◼ 個人消費はサービスが増加したものの、財が減少した影響の方が大きかったと予想す
る。設備投資は 4-6 月期に特殊要因で減少していた反動もあって増加したとみられる。
公共投資は減少、政府消費は増加したと見込んでいる。 
◼ 輸出と輸入はサービスが減少した一方、財が増加したと予想する。代替調達の進展に
よる鉱物性燃料輸入増加は実質 GDPを押し下げたが、石油国家備蓄の大規模放出が行
われなかったことで公的在庫変動が実質 GDP成長率を押し上げた分が相殺したようだ。

図表 1：2026年 7-9月期 GDP予測表

実質国内総生産(GDP) 前期比％ ▲ 0.4 0.3 0.5 0.4 0.4

1．2026年 7-9月期の実質 GDP成長率は前期比年率＋1.5％を予想 
11月 16日公表予定の 2026年 7-9月期の GDP1次速報では、実質 GDPが前期比年率＋1.5％
（前期比＋0.4％）と、4四半期連続のプラス成長を予想する（10月30日正午までに公表された
基礎統計に基づく）。個人消費は小幅に減少したが、設備投資と輸出が実質GDPを押し上げたよ
うだ。 
個人消費はサービスが増加したものの、財が減少した影響の方が大きかったと予想する。設
備投資は4-6月期に特殊要因で減少していた反動もあって増加したとみられる。 
輸出と輸入はサービスが減少したものの、財が増加したと予想する。中東産原油の代替調達
の進展による鉱物性燃料輸入の増加は実質 GDPを押し下げたが、石油国家備蓄の大規模放出が
行われなかったことで公的在庫変動が実質GDP成長率を押し上げた分で相殺されたようだ。

図表 2：実質 GDP成長率と需要項目別寄与度の推移

民需：個人消費、住宅投資は減少の一方、設備投資は大幅な増加を予想 
個人消費は前期比▲0.1％と予想する。4-6月期のインバウンド（個人消費の控除項目）の実
績が上方改定されるであろうことを踏まえると、2 四半期連続のマイナスとなると見込んでい
る。サービスは増加したものの、耐久財と半耐久財、非耐久財がそろって減少したとみている。
耐久財では、自動車税の環境性能割廃止やエアコンの省エネ基準の引き上げの影響もあって 4-
6 月期に増加していた自動車や家電への支出が減少したようだ。半耐久財・非耐久財では衣服
やたばこ、ガソリンなどが増加したものの、食料品などが減少したとみられる。サービスは外
食や鉄道旅客、娯楽などが増加したようだ。 
住宅投資は前期比▲1.8％と予想する。国土交通省によると、7月の建設工事費デフレーター
（住宅総合、大和総研による季節調整値）が前月比＋6.8％と高い伸びを示す中にあっても、7、
8 月平均の新設住宅着工戸数（国土交通省による季節調整値）は、分譲住宅の増加に支えられ
-1.0

### Page 3

1

3 / 7

4-6月平均比＋1.0％となった（巻末の関連指標②上段右）。しかし、進捗ベースで計上される
GDP統計上は、建設資材等の価格上昇の影響により実質では減少したとみられる。 
設備投資は前期比＋2.0％と予想する。建設投資は減少したものの、機械投資と知的財産生
産物投資（ソフトウェア、研究開発など）が増加したようだ。研究開発投資の増加には、4-6
月期に特殊要因で研究開発サービスの受取（設備投資の控除項目）が大幅に増加したことの反
動も含まれる。経済産業省によると、資本財総供給指数は、国産品と輸入品が共に増加し、7-
9 月期は前期比＋4.9％となった（巻末の関連指標②中段左）。一方、建設投資は、国土交通省
によると、民間非居住用建築・土木の出来高（名目額、大和総研による季節調整値）の7、8月
平均は 4-6月平均比＋1.2％となったが、7月の建設工事費デフレーター（非住宅総合、大和総
研による季節調整値）は前月比＋7.3％と大きく上昇しており、実質では減少したとみられる。 
民間在庫変動は前期比寄与度▲0.0％ptと予想する。仮置き値の原材料在庫は同＋0.1％pt、
仕掛品在庫は同▲0.1％pt となった。その他では製品在庫が同＋ 0.1％pt、流通在庫が同▲
0.1％ptだったとみられる。

公需：公共投資は減少、政府消費は増加と予想 
公共投資は前期比▲1.0％と予想する。国土交通省によると、7、8 月平均の公共工事出来高
（名目額、大和総研による季節調整値）は 4-6月平均比▲2.2％と減少傾向にある（巻末の関連
指標②中段右）。25年度補正予算に盛り込まれた公共事業の執行が一服したとみられる。加え
て、工事費の上昇も重しとなっている。 
政府消費は前期比＋0.2％と予想する。退職金支払いの増加や私立高校授業料の実質無償化、
公立小学校の給食費無償化もあって高い伸びとなった 4-6月期に続き、医療費の増加により 7-
9月期の政府消費も増加したとみられる。 
公的在庫変動は前期比寄与度＋0.3％ptと予想する。4-6月期と異なり、7-9月期には大規模
な石油の国家備蓄放出は行われず、公的在庫変動は実質GDP成長率を押し上げたとみられる。

外需：輸入増が輸出増を上回り、外需寄与度は▲0.2％ptを予想 
輸出は前期比＋0.5％と予想する。サービスは減少したものの、財は増加したと見込んでい
る。財では半導体等製造装置や化学製品、鉱物性燃料などが増加したようだ。サービスでは、
4-6月期に大幅に増加していた研究開発サービスの反動減に加え、産業財産権等使用料やイン
バウンドなども減少したとみている。 
輸入は前期比＋1.8％と予想する。輸出と同様、サービスは減少したものの、財は増加した
と見込んでいる。財では、代替調達の進展により鉱物性燃料が大きく増加したほか、電算機類
も増加したようだ。サービスではアウトバウンドは増加したものの、研究開発サービスや著作
権等使用料などが減少したとみられる。

### Page 4

1

4 / 7

輸入増が輸出増を上回り、純輸出（外需）の実質GDP成長率に対する寄与度は前期比▲0.2％
ptになったと予想する。

2．10-12月期の実質 GDPはおおむね横ばいを予想 
2026年 10-12月期の実質 GDP成長率は前期比でおおむね横ばいになると見込んでいる。個人
消費は小幅に増加、設備投資は増加すると予想する。輸出はAI関連需要を背景とした米国向け
輸出を中心に増加するものの、輸入の伸びが上回り、外需はマイナスになるとみている。 
個人消費は小幅に増加すると予想する。厚生労働省「毎月勤労統計調査」の現金給与総額は
前年同月比で 3％を超える伸びが続いていることに加え、10月以降は各地で最低賃金が引き上
げられることから、名目賃金の高い伸びは続くだろう。一方、9月の消費者物価指数（除く生
鮮食品）は前年同月比＋2.1％と、8月から 0.4％pt伸び幅が拡大した。政策要因による影響を
差し引いたとしても、食料品等を中心に物価上昇率は高まっており、実質賃金上昇率を押し下
げるだろう。エネルギー価格についても、電気・ガス料金補助は12月使用分（1月請求分）か
ら再開されるものの、現行の補助は 9月使用分（10月請求分）で一旦終了するため、10-12月
期の物価は押し上げられる。加えて、ガソリン補助金の基準価格が12月以降引き上げられるこ
とや、燃料費・原料費調整制度によって資源価格高騰の影響がラグを持って電気・ガス料金に
反映されることも物価の押し上げ要因となろう1。 
設備投資は増加すると予想する。日本銀行「全国企業短期経済観測調査」 （日銀短観）によ
ると、9月調査時点での 2026年度の設備投資計画（全規模全産業、除く土地、含むソフトウェ
ア・研究開発）は前年度比＋9.3％と、前年の 9月調査時点（同＋9.5％）と同程度の伸びが示
された。建設資材価格と労務費の上昇が建設投資を下押しするものの、省力化投資やAI関連投
資といった設備投資の増加基調は続くと見込む。 
住宅投資は建設資材と労務費上昇の影響を受けて減少すると見込んでいる。公共投資は工事
費の高騰を背景に横ばい圏で推移すると予想する。政府消費は高齢化などに伴う医療費・介護
費増により増加すると見込んでいる。 
輸出は増加すると予想する。米国の設備投資は中東情勢をめぐる不透明感や金利上昇の中に
あっても、AI関連需要を背景に増加が続くだろう。米国内での設備投資の増加は米国向け輸出
の追い風となり、財輸出は増加すると見込んでいる。一方、インバウンドは中国政府による日
本への渡航自粛要請が続く中、小幅な増加にとどまるだろう。 
輸入は増加すると予想する。経済産業省によると、9月の原油調達について、バブ・エル・
マンデブ海峡経由で調達予定であった原油の輸送ルートをスエズ運河経由に切り替えたことで

1 12月以降の電気・ガス料金補助とガソリン補助金に関する政府の対応については、首相官邸「中東情勢を踏
まえた『緊急的激変緩和措置』の今後の対応についての会見」（2026年10月 29日）を参照。

### Page 5

1

5 / 7

到着が遅れた分は備蓄原油の既放出分で対応したという2。10月は前年平月比 10割の調達が実
現できる見通しとのことであり、11月以降も同量を確保できるのであれば、財輸入は押し上げ
られるだろう。 
リスク要因は中東情勢の悪化とAI関連需要の下振れである。中東情勢が悪化し、原油価格が
さらに高騰すれば、物価高を通じて個人消費を一段と下押しするだろう。また、足元の米国経
済では設備投資が景気を下支えしており、とりわけデータセンターや半導体などのIT関連投資
がけん引役となっている。10月半ばにかけて、一部AI関連企業の財務上の脆弱性が意識され、
株価が下落する局面があったが、こうした懸念がAI関連企業全体に広がれば、外部資金への依
存度が高い企業を中心に設備投資が鈍化し得る3。その場合、米国向け輸出が下押しされる可能
性があり、注意が必要だ。

2 詳細は、第 5回中東情勢に伴う重要物資の安定的な供給確保のためのタスクフォース（2026年 10月2日）
における経済産業省提出資料を参照。 
3 詳細は、矢作大祐・藤原翼「米国経済見通し CoreWeave問題をどうみるか」（大和総研レポート、2026年
10月22日）を参照。

Chroma ATE (2360.TW)
05 November 2026 Citi Research
3
Chroma ATE
Valuation
Our target price of NT$2,900 is based on 40x 2027E P/E, at close to 1x PEG. Chroma shares have re-rated above its historical
range as its growth outlook is now strongly tied to AI infrastructure build out. We estimate 60%+ of its revenue are now AI-
related, with strong growth across product segments including power testers, SLT, metrology, and photonics. Chroma has a
commanding market share in most of these segments and well positioned to capitalized on the global AI expansion.

Risks
Downside risks to achieving our target price include: 1) softer AI/CPO growth expectation due to changes in capex outlook or
production bottleneck; 2) fewer SLT needed as a result of shortened cycle time as GPU yield rate improves; 3) disappointing
metrology shipment due to changes at ramp-up schedule; 4) testing process change leading to loss in market share; 5) FX risks
(50-60% of Chroma’s sales are in US$, 10-20% in Rmb).
Prepared for Kevin Lu

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

3Q26 Missed on Margins
No strong near-term catalysts despite trough valuation.
3Q26  results:
• Off revenue of NT$9,936mn (-3% QoQ/-5% YoY), GM declined 6.5ppt QoQ
to 52.2%, significantly below MSe at 57.6%/consensus at 58.0%. We think
this is probably due to less-favorable revenue mix.
• Operating profit of NT$2,643mn (OPM 26.6%) was 21% lower than MSe at
NT$3,330mn (OPM 33.4%)/19% off consensus at NT$3,281mn (OPM 33.6%).

• Net profit  at NT$2,652mn (EPS of NT$2.31) was 24% below MSe at NT
$3,491mn (EPS NT$3.03)/20% off consensus at NT$3,307mn (EPS NT$2.79).
October revenue was NT$3,747mn (+4% MoM/+74% YoY):
• 33% of both our 4Q26 revenue estimate at NT$11,459mn (+15% QoQ/+63%
YoY) and  consensus of NT$11,263mn (+13% QoQ/+61% YoY).
Other announcements (see more in Other announcements ):
•   E Ink announced spending of NT$2.78bn acquiring the land and factories in
Guanyin (Taiwan) from its related party Chunghwa Pulp  for its large-size

signage capacity.
• It  partners with Taiflex  for the latter to do CCL pilot production at E Ink's
facility, with potentially room for collaboration on mass production in the
future.
• It announced it would invest NT$187mn in the rights issuance from its
related party SinoPac FHC.
Our view:
• 4Q26 revenue will need to grow by 10% QoQ to put overall 2026 revenue at
10% YoY (low end of its guidance at 10-15% YoY), which implies Nov-26 and
Dec-26 could be down low single digits % MoM each. While it still looks

within reach, we suspect this is at least partially riding the tailwinds of more
module shipments, which might continue to suppress its gross margin.
• Although the  valuation is at a trough level of 11x 2027e P/E, we see no
strong catalysts in the near term except for the potential fund flows amid
the subdued AI appetite. That said, we remain a believer of longer-term e-
paper demand across various applications.
• 3Q26 earnings call is scheduled at 2:30pm HKT on November 12.

3Q26  results
• Off revenue of NT$9,936mn (-3% QoQ/-5% YoY), gross margin declined by 6.5ppt
QoQ to 52.2%, significantly below MSe at 57.6% and consensus at 58.0%. We think
this is probably owing to a less-favorable revenue mix.
• Opex was NT$2,548mn (+4% QoQ/+16% YoY; opex ratio of 25.6%) vs. slightly
higher than MSe at NT$2,416mn (opex ratio 24.2%) and consensus at NT$2,386mn
(opex ratio 24.4%).
• Operating profit of NT$2,643mn (OPM 26.6%) was 21% lower than MSe at NT

$3,330mn (OPM 33.4%) and 19% below consensus at NT$3,281mn (OPM 33.6%).
• With smaller non-operating income of NT$406mn, net profit came in at NT
$2,652mn (EPS of NT$2.31), 24% lower than MSe at NT$3,491mn (EPS of NT$3.03)
and 20% below consensus at NT$3,307mn (EPS of NT$2.79).

Valuation Methodology and Risks
E Ink Holdings Inc. (8069.TWO)
Base case, 12x 2027e P/E. We believe this valuation methodology best captures E Ink's earn-
ings growth momentum. We view our target multiple of 12x as fair, considering our estimated
17% earnings CAGR for 2025-28. The implied PEG of 0.7x is largely in line with its typical level
in 2022-25, when the stock was trading at an average P/E of 22x one-year forward consensus
EPS with a 37% earnings CAGR.

Risks to Upside
n Firmer-than-expected ESL orders
n Stronger demand for e-readers and e-notes
n Better macro conditions
n Milder competition
n Brisker inventory digestion
Risks to Downside
n Softer-than-expected ESL orders
n Slower demand for e-readers and e-notes
n Macro headwinds
n Intensified competition
n More protracted inventory digestion

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

Analyst Industry Views

below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Greater China Technology Semiconductors
Asia Pacific
Industry View Attractive
Greater China Semiconductors  | Asia Pacific
Cloud Semis:  Peak Growth, Less
Upside
We turn cautious on cloud semis as our US team expects ~20%

of 2028 US data center capacity additions could shift to 2029,
potentially impacting cloud semis suppliers' 2028 earnings.
What’s Changed
Montage Technology Co Ltd (6809.HK) From To
Rating Overweight Equal-weight
Price Target HK$432.00 HK$207.00
Montage Technology Co Ltd (688008.SS)
Rating Overweight Equal-weight
Price Target Rmb377.00 Rmb180.00
Aspeed Technology (5274.TWO)
Rating Overweight Equal-weight
Price Target NT$24,000.00 NT$14,417.00
2028 capex under the spotlight. We turned neutral on cloud semi suppliers in

September 2024 on capex/revision peaking risks and GB200 execution concerns,
then upgraded Aspeed in June 2025 as revisions approached a trough and rack-level
execution improved (Cloud semis: time to turn constructive). AI agents reinforced
our conviction in inference demand.  In our recent note, Cloud Semis: Growing Cloud
Capex into 2027, we remained constructive  on our Cloud Semis coverage due to
ongoing hyperscaler capex growth.  However, after the US midterms, we are mindful

of delays in project approvals and initiation, and existing labor and material
constraints, in terms of what that could mean for hyperscalers' 2028 capex, and the
build-out and capex cycle.   Therefore, we see rising risks  over the next 2-4 quarters
for our coverage; earnings growth should provide support, but further multiple
expansion may become more difficult, as the direction of growth matters more,
even as absolute growth remains strong.
Cut Montage & Aspeed to EW. From our Asia supply-chain perspective, peaking

revision breadth and greater uncertainty on 2027/28 general-server production
warrants more conservative forecasts. Aspeed: we continue to like the stock from a
longer-term perspective given its strong supply chain positioning but see limited
rerating potential. Montage: we see potentially slower China procurement and
derating risk; structural content growth remains supportive, but exposure reduction
is preferable before deceleration. Upside risks include a resumption of earnings

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
Revisions look mature: Earnings-revision breadth is peaking; Asia supply-chain
visibility on 2027/28 general-server production is weakening.
Reduce exposure: Lower Montage estimates; downgrade Aspeed/Montage to EW.
November 9, 2026 04:00 PM GMT

### Page 2

M
Idea
2
The cloud capex cycle is being impacted by the US midterms: Our US team now expects a ~20% of 2028 US data center capacity
additions to shift to 2029,  including some projects in Texas and Ohio, as governors introduce new data center legislation (Buy the Drop: AI
Investment Opportunities Post the US Midterms by Stephen Byrd). Stephen has shifted ~5GW of US data center capacity additions from
2028 to 2029  (Exhibit 1 ) (equivalent to 20% of US and 10% of global 2028 capacity additions). This implies US data center GW additions

decline 4% Y/Y in 2028 before rising 38% Y/Y in 2029, while global data center additions increase 9% and    20% Y/Y in 2028 and 2029.
Our US team believes this could create an air pocket across the tech supply chain in 2028, potentially reducing pricing power and leading
to a inventory build. Despite potential project delays, our US team maintains its  hyperscaler capex forecasts (Buy the Drop: AI Investment
Opportunities Post the US Midterms) at US$1.6trn/US$1.8trn in 2027/28 ( Exhibit 4 ), as it  expects the hyperscalers to spend and build as

much capacity as they can, while attempting to adjust their build locations to other states that are more data center friendly (like
Tennessee, Louisiana, and others).

Aspeed: Valuation Methodology
We cut our price target (base case scenario value) from   NT$24,000 to NT$14,417: This
primarily reflects lower growth assumptions, alongside earnings revisions. We reduce our
terminal growth rate from 5.5% to 3.0% to better align with macro growth outlook, and
reduce our intermediate growth rate from 21.4% to 18.0% to reflect a potential slowdown
in market demand.   Other key assumptions are unchanged, including:

• Cash payout ratio of 85%
• Cost of equity of 9.8% (2% risk-free rate, 6% risk premium, 1.3 beta)
Our bull and bear case scenario values  also decrease from NT$29,300 and NT$11,500, to
NT$17,600 and NT$6,900, respectively, implying 51x/20x our 2027e EPS estimates.

Morgan Stanley Research 11
Montage: Valuation Methodology
We cut our A-share price target to Rmb180 from Rmb377: The change reflects our
lower earnings estimates. Our price target is our base case value, derived from a residual
income (RI) model, which we believe best captures the company's sustainable long-term
growth.  We also cut our intermediate growth rate estimate from 19.3% to 13.6% to reflect
weaker downstream demand estimates. Other RI model assumptions are unchanged,

including a 8.4% cost of equity (beta 1.2, risk-free rate 3.0%, and risk premium 4.5%), a
payout ratio of 30%, and a terminal growth rate of 4.0%. We apply an updated exchange
rate of HK$1.15 : Rmb1, assuming no H-share premium or discount to the A-share, to derive
our H-share price target, which we lower to HK$207 from HK$432.
For the A-share, we cut our bull case value to Rmb218 from Rmb472, and our bear case
value to Rmb83 from Rmb171. For the H-share, we cut our bull case value to HK$259 from

HK$540, and our bear case value to HK$94 from HK$196. These revisions are in line with
our base case value changes.
Exhibit 13: Montage RI Valuation

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

## Document 155: 261110_daiwa_gce

Source PDF: all report pdf/261110_daiwa_gce.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-10
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: We review GCE’s 3Q26 results, released on 10 November.

What’s the impact: 3Q26 beat Bloomberg consensus. GCE’s 3Q26 
earnings came in at TWD6.39bn (EPS: TWD12.4), in line with our estimate 
and 3% higher vs. the street’s. While revenue was 7% lower than our 
expectation due to flooding in Thailand and product transition, gross margin 
expanded by 4pp QoQ to 38.7% mainly due to easing raw-material cost 
inflation and its own price increases. However, net income was impacted by 
higher non-op loss of TWD238m (likely due to FX loss) though offset by a 
lower effective tax rate of 25% as Thailand plant turned more profitable.

Business outlook. Looking ahead, GCE now guides for revenue to be 
flattish QoQ in 4Q26. We suspect that AMZN’s ASIC AI server gradually 
shifting its model from air-cooled (2 chipsets per mainboard) to liquid-
cooled (4 chipsets) and the year-end inventory-taking are the key reasons. 
However, we expect the stagnant revenue momentum to be short-lived and 
growth momentum to resume with new projects (including Google’s TPU 
and MSFT’s Maia) contributing from 1H27.

In 2H27, we expect new capacity ramps at its 2nd plant in Thailand and 2nd 
plant in Suzhou (acquisition of Pegatron’s land; across the 1st plant in 
Suzhou) to be growth drivers, which could further increase its allocation in 
major ASIC AI server projects. As for concerns over the thicker-HDI board 
(a combination of sequential lamination HLC and HDI together) that we 
highlighted in in our previous note (2Q26 results review: profitability missed 
slightly), we believe GCE has already overcome the difficulties for 3-
lamination HDI and can now successfully produce AMZN’s requirement on 
its Trn4 AI server heavy HDI board. However, GCE might still need to 
achieve the requirement for Google’s TPU v9 which requires c.5-6 
lamination techniques. We believe the capacity expansion in 2027 would 
not be enough to catch up with AI demand growth in 2027. As such, we 
believe it will further drive price hikes in CCL raw-material, CCL, and PCB, 
and see gross margin (GM) expansion over our forecast horizon. We raise 
our GM forecasts to 40.5%/41.3% (prior: 38.0/40.2%) for 2027-28E.

What we recommend: We raise our 2027-28E EPS by 1-10% to reflect its 
3Q26 results and higher GM on current supply-demand dynamics. We 
reaffirm our Buy (1) call but trim our 12M TP to TWD1,600 with a lower 
PER target of 21x (prior: 26x; vs. past-3-year range of 7-28x), on our 4-
quarter-forward diluted EPS, to reflect weaker market sentiment and on-
going concerns over HDI. Key risk: worse-than-expected AI server demand.

How we differ: Our 2027-28E earnings are 4-5% higher than consensus, 
likely due to our higher profitability assumptions.

10 November 2026

3Q26 review: results largely in line with our estimates

➢ GM expands with spec upgrade and favourable pricing environment 
➢ New client and new products likely to be key growth drivers in 2027 
➢ Maintaining Buy (1) rating; trimming 12M TP to TWD1,600

Company profile 
Founded in 1981 and listed on the Taiwan Stock Exchange in 1998, Gold Circuit Electronics (GCE) was, 
in its early years, one of the world’s top-3 printed circuit board (PCB) vendors providing double-sided 
and multi-layer PCBs for notebooks. However, as the notebook market became saturated from 2011, 
GCE started reshuffling its revenue mix to more high-end products (servers, workstations and 
networking boards). The requirement of higher-layer PCBs from these products has not only proved 
GCE’s manufacturing capabilities but also created a high entry barrier. GCE’s revenue contribution from 
the server and networking segments has surged to over 80% in 2024 (from 37% in 2010), the highest 
among its PCB peers.

### Page 5

5

Gold Circuit Electronics (2368 TT): 10 November 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 10 Nov 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 2
GCE's Board consists of 9 directors, including 3 independent directors. The number of
independent directors is above Taiwan securities law's requirement of more than two and
exceeds one-fifth of the total number of directors on the board. The backgrounds of the
independent directors are diverse with expertise in the areas of business management, finance
& audit, and law, which should be positive for executive and board quality.
That said, the roles of CEO and chairman are held by one person, who is a second-generation
family member of GCE’s founder. The organisation structure appears to us to be a family
business, which could lead to conflicts of interest between key executives and outside
shareholders.
Capital management 2
GCE declared a DPS of TWD10.0 in 2025, another record high. GCE has declared cash
dividends for six consecutive years from 2020 to 2025, following a period of intermittent payouts
mainly due to fierce competition in the PCB industry (especially in notebook applications) and
high earnings volatility from its 3 plants in China caused by historically low utilisation rates. We
believe the company’s strategy to focus on high-end products (eg, servers and networking) has
started bearing fruit, which was also the main reason GCE was able to declare a record-high
cash dividend in 2025. Overall, we expect GCE to continue to benefit from ASP and gross-
margin expansion, driven by server and networking spec upgrades, which should be value-
accretive for investors in the long term.
Related party &
transaction 1 Sales to related parties were 0% of total 2025 sales and purchases from related parties were also 0% of total 2025 purchases. We see limited risk from related-party transactions.
S Supply chain management 1
GCE is a multi-layer PCB manufacturer with major raw materials, such as thin core, copper foil,
pre-preg, copper ball, dry film, solder mask and various chemical materials used in the
manufacturing process. GCE has acquired the ISO 9001 and IECQ QC 080000 certifications to
improve its supply chain management capabilities in product quality and hazardous material
usage. Like most hardware vendors, GCE is also committed to refusing procurement of conflict
minerals and complying with the Responsible Business Alliance (RBA) Code of Conduct.
According to its 2025 annual report, over 99% of its top 40 suppliers returned their RBA
commitment letters. As part of its RBA compliance efforts, GCE holds an annual supply chain
meeting to review the achievement rate of its previous targets and set new goals for suppliers. In
2025, GCE audited 154 suppliers, including 114 on-site audits and 40 document reviews,
achieving a 100% completion rate against its audit targets. The company also evaluates new
suppliers based on ISO 9001, ISO 14001 and IATF 16949 certification requirements and
conducts annual supplier assessments.
S Data security 2
Information security is one of the key topics under the company’s risk management guidelines.
GCE constructed an information security system, under the “information security committee”, to
minimise the risk of information loss, sabotage, and leakage caused by human errors, malicious
destructions or natural disasters. Below are several measures the company has taken to
improve data security: GCE will conduct regular awareness training to ensure employees'
security awareness and fulfilment of information security system requirements. Also, the
information security committee will develop an information security continuity plan and conduct
drills to ensure continuous operation of the company.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

GS Forecast  __________________________________

GS Factor Profile  ______________________________

AWSC’s 3Q26 core business OP was NT$209mn, which was lower 
than both GSe/BBG consensus at NT$226mn/NT$250mn, mainly 
due to the ongoing increase in raw material cost, leading to a 24.3% 
GM (lower than GSe/BBG consensus GM at 25.6%/27.8%), despite 
UTR in 3Q26 remaining stable at 65-70%. Moreover, AWSC reported 
NI of NT$191mn, which was 3% higher than GSe but 17% lower than 
the BBG consensus due to the GM contraction. 
Going into 4Q26, we believe the company’s revenue will increase by 
1% as key business lines (including WiFi, cellular and VCSEL) should 
remain stable in 4Q26 as we expect the company’s utilization rate to 
remain stable at 65-70%. Moreover, we expect its GM to rise to 
27.2% (up 2.9ppt QoQ) due to the material cost increase slowing 
down. 
We continue to view rising material costs as the key risk to AWSC’s 
profitability, as the company is now securing raw materials at higher 
cost as low-cost inventory has already been depleted in 1H26 amid 
the strong demand pull in. The company continues to negotiate 
price increases with clients and expects the adjustments to be fully 
reflected starting in 1Q27. However, we remain cautious on the 
low-end cellular segment, where customer demand tends to be 
more price-sensitive. 
For the AI-related business, the company continues to ship VCSEL 
products for 400G/800G optical transceiver this year, with revenue 
contribution to be <2% in 2026E per our estimates. Meanwhile, the 
company continues to make progress on the new PD (Photodiode) 
product and is currently undergoing verification with several 
customers. The company expects PD products to start seeing 
revenue contribution starting from 2H27 or 2027-end given its long 
verification process, typically 6 months+, thus we expect revenue 
contribution to be limited at <2%/~5% in 2027E/2028E. 
Investment view

### Page 3

We maintain our conservative view on the cellular/WiFi PA industry (see here) as we 
continue to see weaker smartphone shipment (-10%/+2%/+2% in 2026/27/28, see 
here) as the smartphone market continues to be impacted by the elevated memory price 
(see here). 
For AWSC, we believe the company could further gain market share among China PA 
design houses as Chinese clients increasingly shift toward higher-end PA products, 
supported by the company’s superior cost control capability and higher production yield 
relative to peers. In addition, AWSC has continued to expand its market share with a 
Korean smartphone brand, which should provide further growth momentum to its 
cellular business in coming quarters/years. Overall, we expect AWSC to continue gaining 
share from its key competitors due to (1) its competitive pricing strategy, underpinned 
by a more favorable cost structure comparing to its key competitor, Win Semi 
(AWSC/Win Semi’s fixed cost accounts for 30%/40% of the total, while Win Semi’s COGS 
per wafer is ~130% higher than AWSC’s) and (2) its strategic focus on China- and 
US-based PA design houses. 
We continue to see a recovery in 2026 CAPEX plans among Taiwan PA foundry players. 
However, we do not view this as a signal of a meaningfully stronger demand outlook as 
the capital spending is more related to new AI businesses. AWSC revised up its 2026 
CAPEX guidance to NT$600-650mn (from NT$500-600mn) and continues to expect 
CAPEX to increase in 2027/28. Also, management continues to expect UTR to reach at 
most ~70% in 2026, implying no need for capacity expansion in the near term while the 
additional capex will be spent on new optical related business. 
Overall, we maintain our Neutral rating on AWSC with a new 12-m TP of NT$136 (from 
NT$147) as we roll over our valuation period by one quarter. Post results, we revise 
down our 2027E/28E earnings by 10%/10% to reflect lower revenue and the negative 
impact from rising material cost (GM contraction), revise up 2026E earnings by 2%, and 
introduce our 2029E estimates.

Earnings revisions  
We revise up our 2026E revenue by 1% to factor in the 3Q26 actual results and we revise 
down 2027E/28E revenue by 9%/10% assuming a more conservative outlook for the 
cellular business; we revise down 2026E/27E/28E GM estimates by 0.4/0.7/0.8ppt to 
reflect the negative impact from rising material cost. Overall, we revise down 2027E/28E 
earnings by 10%/10% and revise up 2026E earnings by 2%. 
In this report, we introduce our 2029E estimates.

Valuation 
We maintain our Neutral rating and revise down our TP from NT$147 to NT$136, based 
on a lower 25x target P/E (from 27x previously, 0.5 STDV below the company’s past 
10-year forward P/E multiple), factoring in our earnings revisions and, consistent with 
our group coverage practices, rolling over our valuation period from 4Q26-3Q27E to 
2027E.

### Page 6

Investment Thesis and TP & risks

AWSC is one of the largest GaAS foundries globally, with 10-15% market share in the 
GaAS foundry industry in 2025. We expect the company to enjoy solid share gain 
opportunity in the long term, considering its much lower than peers’ production cost 
and better production efficiency. However, given the high inventory level in the supply 
chain, and the slower-than-expected Android smartphone PA demand in coming 
quarters, we expect relatively slow profit growth in the near term. 
We are Neutral rated given the company’s relatively slow profit growth in coming 
quarters. We think the stock is priced fairly, trading at its historical upcycle average P/E 
multiple, despite the long-term market share growth opportunity. We forecast AWSC’s 
net income to grow at a 34% 2026E-28E CAGR. The key up/downside risks are (1) 
easing/rising competition in the China PA foundry market, (2) 
faster/slower-than-expected progress on 5G/WiFi 7 upgrade trends, and (3) 
faster/slower-than expected progress with new businesses, including LEO satellite, 
drones and datacom.  
Valuation methodology: Our 12-month target price of NT$136 is based on a target 25x 
2027E P/E, which is 0.5 STDV below the company’s past 10-year forward P/E multiple 
and the inventory build-up period average P/E. We consider this multiple appropriate 
given the soft demand outlook and our positive view on the company’s longer-term 
share gain opportunity. 
The key up/downside risks are 1) easing/rising competition in the China PA foundry 
market, (2) faster/slower-than-expected progress on 5G/WiFi 7 upgrade trends, and (3) 
faster/slower-than expected progress with new businesses, including LEO satellite, 
drones and datacom.

return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

世芯為 IC 設計服務廠商：世芯於 2003 年成立，提供客戶 ASIC 與 SoC 的委託設
計服務，並協助客戶進行後端晶圓製造與封裝測試流程。世芯 3Q26 終端應用以
HPC 為重(佔比 87%)、消費電子 8%(比如數位相機、平板電腦與影像處理)、網路
通訊與利基型應用(包括娛樂機台、保全系統等)總計 5%。由於 HPC 採先進製程進
行，故世芯營收以製程別而言，高度集中於7nm 以下，7nm 以下營收佔比 89%。
世芯主攻北美市場，營收佔比 74%，其餘則分布於日本台灣等地。國內相關 IC 設
計服務公司包括創意與智原。 
3Q26 獲 利 優 於 預 期 ：世 芯公布 3Q26 營收 8.19 億美元 (+239%QoQ ，
+267%YoY)， 毛利率18%， 營業利益率13.9%， 稅後獲利1.02 億美元(+97%QoQ，
+131%YoY)， 稅後EPS 37.79 元 ， 獲利優於預期(研究部原預估稅後 EPS 34.68 元)，
主要是 AWS T3 挹注。世芯 3Q26 財報細項表現如下：(1) 美元營收季增 239%，
主要是 AWS T3 放量；(2) 毛利率由 2Q26 的 34.8%下滑至 18%，主要是量產比
重攀升，數個 NRE 專案遞延至 4Q26 所致。受限供應鏈緊俏(包括晶圓/載板/封裝
產能)，難免排擠承接其餘高毛利率產品的程度，使得 HPC 比重由前季的 50%飆
升至 87%；(3) 營業費用僅季增 4%， 反映營運槓桿效益顯現 。 整體而言 ， 世芯3Q26
財報顯示：隨 AWS T3 放量，公司(1) 營收 87%集中於 HPC。(2) 北美營收比重
由前季的 51%擴增至 84%，(3) 3nm/2nm 製程由前季的 47%攀升至 80%。 
4Q26 營收持續增溫：世芯表示(1) 4Q26 AWS T3 晶片挹注>3Q26，(2) AWS T4
進入 final implementation 階段 ， 預計4Q27 步入量產 。 整體而言 ， 公司預期4Q26
營收隨量產規模提升與 NRE 案件挹注而續增，毛利率與 3Q26 相仿(全年毛利率
>20%)，長期營業利益率 15%~19%(high teens) 。研究部預估世芯 4Q26 營收
344.72 億元 (+31%QoQ，+629%YoY)，毛利率 18%，稅後獲利 42.43 億元
(+30%QoQ，+186%YoY)，稅後 EPS 49.17 元。 
未來動能由於 AWS 下代晶片挹注、新增第二北美客戶：世芯營運自 2026 年重啟
成長態勢 ：(1) 隨今年 AWS T3 自五月底~六月初開始步入量產 ， 世芯2026~2027
年營收將主要由該案引領，公司預期該案 2027 年營收可望呈現三位數成長。世芯
認為示 T3 對公司 2027 年挹注更大 ， 主要是客戶給予公司更多晶圓配置 ；(2) AWS 
T4 維持預計 4Q27 量產，預計 2028 年進入大量生產階段，帶動 2028 年營運規
模向上提升。目前 T3 為 SoC 設計搭配 2.5D 封裝，而 T4 為 chiplet 架構與 GAA
製程，世芯負責個別晶片包括 compute die/IO die 等實體設計與最終封裝設計與
整合 ， 承擔腳色更為吃重 。(3) 第二個客戶為全新客戶(北美)預計 2Q27 開始量產 ，
可望成為第二大營收來源，公司期待該客戶為 10 億美元等級的業務。公司並期待
2027 年隨 AWS 與該位客戶挹注，2027 年整體營收可望呈現三位數成長。(4) 隨
AWS 今明年挹注、第二大客戶明年步入量產，不排除世芯2026 年營收 22 億~23

營運現況與分析 
圖一：世芯產品與客戶區域結構

資料來源：公司；永豐投顧研究部預估整理，Nov. 2026

億美元，2027 年營收 40 億~50 億美元。(4) 長期營業利益率目標 15%~19%。 
維持買進建議：隨主要客戶步入大規模量產階段，研究部預估世芯 2026 年營收
724.92 億元(+134%YoY)，毛利率 21.6%，稅後獲利 105.7 億元(+89%YoY)，稅

174.08 元)。 隨世芯2Q~3Q26 啟動新一輪營運成長 ，2026~2027 年均顯著成長 ，
維持買進建議，不過近期整體市場評價受調整，研究部遂亦調整世芯本益比，故目
標價由 5570 元調整至 4535 元(由 32X 改以 22X2027 年 EPS 評價)。

股 價 相 對 大 盤 走 勢

緯穎 (6669  TT)  Trading Buy

雖預期 伺 服 器 出 貨 量持續 成長 ， 惟 現 階 段 AI 前 景 疑 慮 變
大， 投資建議 Trading Buy 。  
投 資 建 議 ： 基於： (1)Trainium 3 平 台 持 續 放 量 ， 接 續 的 液 冷 版
本 ASP 將 顯 著 高 於 氣 冷 版 本 ，(2) 預期 4Q26 Vera Rubin 和 Helios
小 量 出 貨，並 於 1H27 放 量，GPU 系 列 將 成 ASIC 外 另 一 成 長 引
擎。(3) 通 用 型 伺 服 器 需 求 強 勁，可 望 延 續 至 2027 。然 而 FY3Q26 
CSP 巨 頭 財 報 公 布 後， 又 逢 美 國 期 中 選 舉 反 資 料 中 心 建 置 的 民
主黨大勝 ， 市 場 對 於 AI 投 資 擴 張 疑 慮 上 升 ， 故 群 益 給 予 緯 穎
Trading Buy 的 投 資 建 議 ， 目 標 價 1,800 元。  
通 用 型 與 ASIC 提 供 營 運 支 撐，預 計 Trainium 3 液冷將於 2027
年 放 量： 根 據 我 們 調 查 ， 預 估2026 年 Trainium 3 液 冷 版 本 出 貨
量 將 略 低 預 期，並 可 如 期 於 1H27 放量 。目 前 緯 穎 的 成 長 基 礎 仍
在 通 用 型 及 ASIC 伺 服 器 ，GPU 新 平 台 則 成 為 未 來 另 一 重 要 成
長引擎。  
Vera Rubin 與  Helios 擴 大 產 品 布 局： 緯 穎 於 10/2026 OCP 中
表 示 其 與 緯 創 為 NVIDIA Vera Rubin NVL72 首批 Pilot Build 
Partners 之 一，將 參 與 驗 證 及 design -in，同 場 展 示 的 AMD Helios
則採用 MI450 系列 GPU 機 櫃 ， 預 期 4Q26 後段 GPU 新 平 台 可
小 量 出 貨 ， 並 於 1H27 放量。  
AI 投 資 疑 慮 上 升： 隨 著 FY3Q26 各 大 主 要 雲 端 廠(CSP) 財 報 公
布 ， 市 場 對 於 其 資 本 支 出 是 否 可 維 持 高 檔 存 在 疑 慮 。 此 外 ， 隨
著 11/2026 美 國 期 中 選 舉 結 束 ， 投 資 人 擔 憂 資 料 中 心 建 置 進 度
亦 可 能 放 緩 ， 後 續 可 能 對 緯 穎 營 運 成 長 動 能 造 成 影 響 。 
群 益 預 估 2027 年 營 收 YoY+69.45% ： 預 估 2027 全 年 營 收
25,968.39 億元， YoY+69.45% ； AI 伺 服 器 較 高 價 的 零 組 件 將 造
成 毛 利 率 稀 釋 ， 毛 利 率 7.74% ， YoY -0.9ppts ，營益率 5.69% ，
YoY -1.3ppts ； 營 業 利 益 1,478.02 億 元 ，YoY+37.9% ；稅 後 淨 利
1,186.47 億 元 ， YoY+38.5% ， EPS 212.25 元。

個股報告

2 群益投顧 
  ESG  
環 境：緯 穎 宣 示「 2030 年 達 成 全 球 100% 再 生 能 源 使 用、2040 年 邁 向 淨 零 排
放 」， 減 碳 目 標 已 通 過 SBTi 審 核 。2025 年 全 球 再 生 能 源 使 用 率 達 80.90% ，
廢 棄 物 回 收 利 用 率 達 82.00% ， 並 透 過 冰 水 主 機 溫 度 優 化 、 生 產 設 備 節 能 及
LED 照 明 汰 換 等 措 施 ， 全 年 節 電 630,678 度 ， 相 當 於 減 少 298.74 公噸 CO₂e
排 放。此 外，公 司 推 動 木 箱 及 棧 板 循 環 使 用，2025 年 減 少 約 2,565 公 噸 廢 棄
物 ， 創 造 約 6.7 億 元 經 濟 效 益 ， 並 逐 步 導 入 內 部 碳 定 價 機 制 ， 將 減 碳 效 益 納
入 投 資 決 策 。 
社 會 ： 緯 穎 重 視 人 才 培 育 、 員 工 福 利 及 社 會 參 與 ， 持 續 推 動 全 球 線 上 學 習 、
專 業 職 能 訓 練 及 多 元 共 融 職 場 。2025 年 全 球 員 工 平 均 受 訓 時 數 達 34 小 時 ，
間 接 人 員 平 均 約 50 小 時 ， 並 透 過 職 涯 發 展 及 員 工 溝 通 機 制 強 化 人 才 留 任 。
對 外 則 積 極 投 入 產 學 合 作 、 實 習 人 才 培 育 、 社 區 關 懷 及 環 境 保 育 ，2025 年
社 會 投 入 金 額 達 2,819 萬 元，並 透 過 緯 穎 永 續 基 金 會 推 動 公 益 活 動，累 計 投
入 全 球 植 樹 57,782 棵 及 珊 瑚 復 育 8,296 株 ， 持 續 擴 大 企 業 社 會 影 響 力 。 
治 理 ： 緯 穎 以 董 事 會 為 公 司 治 理 最 高 機 構 ， 設 有 審 計 委 員 會 、 薪 資 報 酬 委
員 會、提 名 委 員 會 及 企 業 永 續 發 展 委 員 會，定 期 檢 視 公 司 治 理 與 永 續 發 展 績
效。公司將 ESG 績 效 納 入 高 階 經 理 人 薪 酬 考 核 ， 並 建 立 風 險 暨 安 全 管 理 機
制，每年至少兩次向審計委員會及董事會報告。此外，依循責任商業聯盟
（ RBA ） 準 則 落 實 誠 信 經 營 與 反 貪 腐 管 理 ，2025 年 全 球 員 工 行 為 準 則 簽 署
率 及 教 育 訓 練 涵 蓋 率 均 達 100% ， 當 年 度 未 發 生 違 反 誠 信 道 德 或 涉 及 貪 腐 之
情事。  
公 司 簡 介 
緯 穎 科 技（6669 TT ）成 立 於 2012 年 ， 為 緯 創 集 團 旗 下 專 注 於 超 大 規 模 資 料
中 心 （Hyperscale Data Center ） 及 雲 端 基 礎 架 構 的 伺 服 器 設 計 與 製 造 商，2019
年 於 台 灣 證 券 交 易 所 掛 牌 上 市。公 司 主 要 提 供 雲 端 運 算、人 工 智 慧 及 資 料 中
心 所 需 之 伺 服 器、儲 存 設 備、網 路 系 統 及 機 櫃 基 礎 設 施，並 提 供 客 製 化 產 品
設 計、系 統 整 合 及 軟 硬 體 解 決 方 案。緯 穎 為 開 放 運 算 計 畫 （OCP ） 白 金 會 員，
主要採 ODM Direct 商 業 模 式 ， 直 接 服 務 全 球 大 型 雲 端 服 務 供 應 商（CSP ），
產 品 涵 蓋 通 用 型 伺 服 器 、AI GPU 伺 服 器 及 客 製 化 ASIC 伺 服 器 ， 並 提 供 L6
主 機 板、L10 整 機 及 L11 整 櫃 等 不 同 層 級 的 系 統 整 合 服 務。公 司 以 台 灣 為 研
發 及 營 運 核 心，於 墨 西 哥、馬 來 西 亞 及 美 國 等 地 布 局 生 產 與 服 務 據 點，主 要
台 灣 同 業 競 爭 對 手 包 括 鴻 海（2317 TT ）、 廣 達（ 2382 TT ）。 2023 年 地 區 別
營 收 比 重 為 ： 美 洲 78.58% 、歐洲 11.90% 、 亞 洲 7.73% 、 其 他 地 區 1.21% 、
台灣內銷 0.57% ， 顯 示 公 司 營 收 高 度 集 中 於 美 洲 市 場 。

.

6 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】 
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15 %

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投 資 評 等 係「 首 次 」給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時，其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較3 個月
目 標 價 之 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 計 算 而 得 。 個 股 投 資 評 等 分 為四 個 等 級 ， 定 義 如 上 。
爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」及 「 區 間 操 作 」均有 upside 目 標 價 。差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一 旦 我 們 給 予 特 定 個 股「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」或「 區 間 操 作 」之 投 資 評 等，
就 是 責 任 的 開 始，爾 後 將 透 過 各 式 研 究 報 告 作 定 期 性、持 續 性 追 蹤 基 本 面 及
股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ， 研
判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 。

【免責聲明】  
  本 研 究 報 告 僅 提 供 予 特 定 人 之 客 戶 作 為 參 考 資 料「 非 經 同 意 不 得 轉 載 」 。我
們 並 不 確 保 此 資 訊 的 完 整 性 與 正 確 性，投 資 人 應 了 解，報 告 中 有 關 未 來 預 測
之 陳 述 可 能 不 會 實 現，因 而 不 應 被 依 賴。而 且 此 報 告 並 非 根 據 特 定 投 資 目 的
或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的，既 非 對
投 資 人 於 買 賣 證 券、選 擇 權、期 貨 或 其 他 證 券 相 關 之 衍 生 性 商 品 提 供 詢 價 服
務，亦 非 作 為 進 行 交 易 的 要 約。投 資 人 應 注 意 到 相 關 證 券 之 價 值 及 收 益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

2026.10.29   
HD현대중공업  (329180)

3Q26: 조선사는 우직해야 한다  Company Brief

3분기 매출 5조 7,536억원/영업이익 8,992억원 
HD현대중공업의 2026년 3분기 매출은 5조 7,536억원/영업이익은 8,992억원으로 
컨센서스(6조 1,295억원/9,671억원) 및 당사 추정치(6조 859억원/9,679억원) 대비 
매출 및 영업이익 각각 -6.1%/-7.0%, -5.5%/-7.1%를 하회하는 실적을 기록하였다.  
조선사업부 매출이 4조 3,988억원/영업이익 6,463억원(OPM 14.7%)이며 이중 상선 
OPM은 14.7%로, 2분기 17.8%에 비해 3.1%P 하락하였으나 1분기 15.9%와 크게 
차이나지 않는 수준을 유지하면서 조업일수 및 환율 하락에도 불구하고 P-MIX및 
빈티지 개선에 따른 효과를 보여주었다. 해양사업부는 매출 3,799억원/영업이익 
1,265억원(OPM 33.3%)로 지난 분기(15.9%)보다도 훨씬 높은 영업이익을 
기록하였다. 엔진기계사업부는 매출 9,511억원/영업이익 2,299억원(OPM 24.2%)를 
기록하며 역시 견조한 실적을 기록하였다.  
일회성 이익으로 Trion 공사의 C/O 180 억원, 일회성 손실로는 셧다운에 따른 
조업감소 영향 500억원 및 환율하락에 따른 이익 감소분 450억원이 있었으며 금액은 
공개되지 않았으나 임단협 잠정타결에 따른 성과급/타결금 지급에 대한 추가 충당금의 
75% 인식이 반영되었다. 알려진 일회성만 제거하더라도 실질 영업이익률은 
17%이상으로 2분기 16.4%보다 높아진 호실적이라고 할 수 있다. 특히 RUYA 공사가 
본 궤도에 오르면서 해양사업부의 이익률이 괄목할 만큼 높아졌으며 지속가능하다는 
회사의 설명이다.

투자의견 BUY 및 조선업종 TOP PICK 유지, 목표주가 530,000원 하향 
HD현대중공업의 목표주가를 530,000원으로 38.3% 하향하지만, 투자의견 BUY와 
조선업종 TOP PICK 을 유지한다. 환율 하락 및 28년 하반기 엔진증설 매출확대, 
해양공사 이익률 상승 등 요인을 종합 반영하여 실적 추정치를 미세 반영하여 도출한 
2027F BPS 131,910원에 Target P/B를 기존대비 하향한 4.0배로 적용하였다. 특수선 
기대감 및 선가상승 기대감 하락에 따른 조선업종 전반의 밸류에이션 하락을 반영하여 
Target을 낮췄지만 그럼에도 조선업종 중 가장 높은 멀티플을 부여하며 Top 
Pick의견을 견지한다. 여러 악재에도 불구하고 HD현대중공업은 탄탄하다. 본업인 
상선사업부의 9월말 기준 수주량은 137.9억 달러로 목표치를 이미 초과 달성하였으며 
전사 기준으로도 수주목표 90.5%를 채워넣었다. 수주잔고 또한 상선 421억 
달러/전사 609억 달러로 각각 작년 말 대비 12.6%/13.2% 증가하였다. 해양부문과 
엔진기계부문은 조선사업부보다도 높은 이익률로 든든한 실적의 기반이 되고 있다.  
최근 HD현대그룹은 미국의 EPC강자 Kiewit과 미국 합작법인 설립에 관한 계약을 
맺고 그린필드 기반의 미국 신규 조선소 건설을 추진하고 있다. 빠르면 올해 중으로 
건립과 관련한 세부 사항이 공개될 예정이며 HD현대중공업이 중추적인 역할을 담당할 
것으로 보인다. 일찍부터 브라운필드 방식으로 접근한 동종사와 달리 다소 시간은 
걸렸지만 과감하게 결정된 투자다. 조선소 예상 준공 역시 2028년 이후로 예상되어 
당장 기업가치에 반영하기는 어려우나 엔진 증설과 함께 2028년 후에는 분명한 실적 
상향 요인이 될 것으로 예상한다. 우직하게 본업에 충실하며 신중하게 미래를 준비하는 
조선소, 귀감이다.

당 보고서 공표일 기준으로 해당 기업과 관련하여, 
 회사는 해당 종목을 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 금융투자분석사와 그 배우자는 해당 기업의 주식을 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 당 보고서는 기관투자가 및 제 3자에게 E-mail등을 통하여 사전에 배포된 사실이 없습니다.  
 회사는 6개월간 해당 기업의 유가증권 발행과 관련 주관사로 참여하지 않았습니다. 
 당 보고서에 게재된 내용들은 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
본 분석자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위한 참고자료이며, 따라서, 본 자료에 의한 투자자의 투자결과에 대해 어떠한 목적의 증빙자료로도 사용될 수 없으며, 어떠한 경우에도 작성자 및 
당사의 허가 없이 전재, 복사 또는 대여될 수 없습니다. 무단전재 등으로 인한 분쟁발생시 법적 책임이 있음을 주지하시기 바랍니다.

[투자의견] 
종목추천 투자등급  
종목투자의견은 향후 12개월간 추천일 종가대비 해당종목의 예상 목표수익률을 의미함.  
 Buy(매수): 추천일 종가대비 +15% 이상  
 Hold(보유): 추천일 종가대비 -15% ~ 15% 내외 등락 
 Sell(매도): 추천일 종가대비 -15% 이상

## Document 268: [373220] LG에너지솔루션(373220) 보상금 제외해도 호실적LG에너지솔루션(373220) 보상금 제외해도 호실적보상금 제외해도 호실적 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[373220] LG에너지솔루션(373220) 보상금 제외해도 호실적LG에너지솔루션(373220) 보상금 제외해도 호실적보상금 제외해도 호실적 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-12
Pages: 10
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

(373220) 
LG 에너지솔루션 
2026.10.12

에너지/인프라/배터리 황성현, Ph.D. 
6878 / tjdgus2009@eugenefn.com

보상금 제외해도 호실적 
 투자의견 BUY(유지) 
 목표주가 530,000원(상향) 
 현재주가 401,000원(10/8)

 [투자의견] 당사는 LG에너지솔루션에 대해 실적 추정치 상향을 반영하여 목
표주가를 530,000원으로 13% 상향하고, 투자의견 BUY를 유지한다. 
3Q26부터 ESS 물량 증가에 따른 가동률 상승 효과가 본격적으로 실적에 
반영되기 시작했으며, 4Q26에도 AMPC 제외 흑자 기조를 이어갈 전망이
다. 전사 가동률은 3분기 65%까지 상승한 것으로 파악되며, 이에 따른 펀
더멘털 개선이 본격화되고 있다고 판단한다. 
 [3Q26 잠정 실적 Review] 매출액 9.6조원(+28%qoq, +59%yoy), 영업이
익 7,560억원(+567%qoq, +26%yoy)을 기록하며 당사 추정을 큰 폭으로 
상회했다. 북미 생산보조금(AMPC)을 제외한 매출액은 9.2조원, 영업이익은 
3,391억원(영업이익률 3.7%)으로, 2 분기(-1,277억원, -1.7%) 대비 흑자 
전환에 성공했다. ① 원통형은 테슬라향 물량 호조에 힘입어 영업이익 
2,010억원(영업이익률 8.1%)을 달성한 것으로 추정된다. ② EV는 유럽 폴
란드 공장의 가동률 상승과 GM 얼티엄의 8월 가동 개시로 매출이 증가했
다. 여기에 완성차 업체 보상금 3,000억원이 반영되며 영업이익 1,090억
원(영업이익률 2.5%)을 기록한 것으로 추정된다. ③ ESS는 매출액이 50% 
이상 급증하며 실적 서프라이즈를 견인했다. AMPC 포함 영업이익은 4,460
억원(영업이익률 16.0%)으로 추정된다. 2 분기 매출 성장률 +17%qoq에 
이어 3분기 성장세가 더욱 가팔라졌다는 점이 긍정적이다. 
 [4Q26 Preview] 매출액 8.0조원(-17%qoq, +30%yoy), 영업이익 6,790
억원(-10%qoq, 흑전yoy)으로 추정한다. 4 분기 실적은 ESS 수익성 개선 
속도와 완성차 업체 보상금 반영 규모에 따라 변동할 전망이다. 동사는 기존 
가이던스를 통해 하반기 북미 ESS 생산량이 상반기 대비 2배 증가할 것으
로 제시한 바 있다. 3분기 ESS는 AMPC를 제외하더라도 양호한 수익성을 
기록한 것으로 추정된다. 향후 팩 생산 병목 해소와 북미 공장 가동 안정화
에 따른 고정비 부담 완화가 이어지며 추가적인 손익 개선이 가능할 전망이
다. EV 역시 GM 얼티엄 생산 확대와 유럽 공장 가동률 상승이 지속되며 
AMPC 제외 적자 규모를 점진적으로 축소할 것으로 예상한다. 
 [투자포인트] 9월 미시간 랜싱 공장의 개소식이 개최됐다. 랜싱 공장은 당초 
EV용 파우치 배터리 생산거점으로 계획됐으나 ESS 중심으로 역할이 재편
됐으며, 내년에는 10GWh 이상의 각형 LFP 생산라인이 추가될 예정이다. 
2027년에는 신규 대규모 증설보다 기존 북미 생산거점의 가동률 상승과 
ESS 생산량 확대를 통한 수익성 개선이 핵심이 될 전망이다. 북미 전력망 
접속 지연과 AI 데이터센터의 부하 변동성 확대에 따라 Stand-alone ESS, 
BTM, UPS, BBU 수요가 증가하면서 신규 수주도 지속 확대될 것으로 예상
한다. 특히 동사의 Non-PFE 요건 충족과 SI· O&M을 아우르는 End-to-
End 솔루션 역량이 북미 ESS 시장에서 경쟁력으로 작용할 전망이다.

당사는 본 자료 발간일을 기준으로 해당종목의 주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다  
당사는 동 자료를 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다 
조사분석담당자는 자료작성일 현재 동 종목과 관련하여 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 조사분석담당자 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다 
동 자료는 당사의 제작물로서 모든 저작권은 당사에게 있습니다 
동 자료는 당사의 동의 없이 어떠한 경우에도 어떠한 형태로든 복제, 배포, 전송, 변형, 대여할 수 없습니다 
동 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료 및 정보로부터 얻어진 것이나, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 어떠
한 경우에 동 자료는 고객의 주식투자의 결과에 대한 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙자료로 사용될 수 없습니다

## Document 303: Blue Wave_ Democrats sweep the US midterms

Source PDF: all report pdf/Blue Wave_ Democrats sweep the US midterms.pdf
Report source: 渣打銀行 (Market Watch)
Date: 2026-11-05
Pages: 10
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Blue Wave: Democrats 
sweep the US midterms  
Summary 
Event: The Democrats gained control of the House of 
Representatives and the Senate at the US midterm elections 
that took place this week.  
Our View: With the US government now no longer under 
unified control of the Republicans, gridlock has returned to 
Washington. We believe investors should not overreact to 
these results and should stay invested. 
In equities, investors should  continue to diversify their 
equity exposure into more defensive sectors.  As we 
transition to mid -cycle for the equity markets , we expect 
stocks to continue to deliver positive returns in the 8 -10% 
range per annum, a downshift from the torrid pace of gains in 
the earlier part of the cycle.  
In bonds, income remains attractive given recent spread 
widening. We favour non -hyperscaler investment grade 
corporate bonds at the three to seven -year part of the 
curve. We continue to expect the US dollar to remain range-
bound in the near  term, as we expect two more rate hikes 
from the Fed, but as this mini rate hike cycle ends in the 
second quarter of 2027, the USD index (DXY) is likely to 
depreciate towards 98.5 as markets start to anticipate a Fed 
rate cut in the second half of next year.  
Oil is likely to rebound in the short -term on heightened 
geopolitical risk, but as supply chains adapt to the current 
situation and Congress exercises oversight on defense 
spending, WTI prices are likely to drift towards our 12-month 
target of USD 80 per barrel.

Democrats made solid gains across the Senate, House 
of Representatives and State Governors 
Results reported as at 7:45PM Singapore Time

Source: The Wall Street Journal, Standard Chartered

### Page 2

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 5 November 2026

Major implications of Democrat control of 
Congress   
1) Fiscal expansionary policies may slow. Democrats are 
now in position to block any new tax cuts or spending plans 
from the Trump administration. At the same time, any fiscal 
policies proposed by Democrats may also face Trump’s veto. 
Hence the fiscal trajectory of the US will likely remain stuck  
over the next two years. As we get into the second half of 
2027, the US debt ceiling will once again loom, and gridlock 
could potentially bring back government shutdowns. 
However, as they have always done in the past, compromise 
will ultimately prevail, in our opinion.  
2) Trump remains in control of foreign policy.  With the 
midterms now out of the way, could Trump decide to re-start 
combat operations with Iran? We believe there is a risk that 
US and Israel – now that both countries’ have concluded 
important elections – will once again be tempted to restart 
combat operations to force an end to the conflict. However, 
the duration of any renewed hostilities may be limited by 
Congress next year as the Democrats will likely withhold 
funding for further military operations. In the near -term, oil 
prices may experience renewed volatility before stabilising at 
a lower level next year. The introduction of new tariffs on 
trading partners may prove harder with Democrat oversight. 
3) AI data centre moratoriums could grow, though model 
development regulatory changes would be harder to 
pass. While the headlines have focused on the House and 
Senate results, one important development has also 
occurred at the gubernatorial level. Notably, Democrats have 
won the governor races in Texas and Georgia, two big data 
centre states. As data centre build out permits are largely 
decided at the local state level, this will have important 
ramifications for build out plans next year.  However, any 
Democratic proposals to regulate AI model developments 
would need to be signed off by President Trump, who 
continues to wield veto power on any such proposals.   
Asset class strategy 
Equities: Remain invested and continue to diversify .  
While investors initially overreacted, President Trump’s 
relatively conciliatory response has provided some welcome 
relief, helping markets recover strongly from their pre-market 
lows. Hence, we recommend investors avoid 
overreacting, remain invested and continue to diversify 
their equity exposure across sectors and regions.  
A few themes stand out from the early pre-market reaction to 
the mid-term elections. On the positive side, defensive stocks 
continue to fare well, while themes favoured by Democrats 
are also outperforming. Clean energy is a notable 
beneficiary, with solar s tocks among the biggest winners

Policy uncertainty and a change in the innovation cycle 
is consistent with our view of a mid-cycle transition, 
where equity returns moderate but remain positive

As we have highlighted in our recent notes on the transition

reinforces the trend. The experience of 2015 -2017, 
highlighted in the second table, offers a useful historical 
parallel. Despite the knee-jerk reaction, investors should not

during the mid -cycle, even amid political headwinds, 
supported by steady underlying earnings growth in both 2027 
and 2028. 
So, what should investors do to navigate the next few months 
before the newly elected representatives are sworn in and 
take control of the House and Senate?  As we have been 
highlighting in recent notes, equity markets should still deliver 
positive returns, albeit at a more moderate pace. Hence, we 
recommend staying invested but diversification remains key  
to navigating the next phase of the cycle.

secondary dollar-negative channel.

strong economy. High yield corporate spreads have widened 
in recent weeks but dispersion wi thin the complex is wide, 
with BBs and Bs credit spreads staying relatively resilient, 
while CCCs have widened to distressed levels. For income -
focused investors, we would recommend staying within the 
crossover range of between BBB-BB.

## Document 376: LatAm’s silicon dividend_ tailwinds from the AI investment boom in the U.S. (2026-10-28)

Source PDF: all report pdf/LatAm’s silicon dividend_ tailwinds from the AI  investment boom in the U.S. (2026-10-28).pdf
Report source: DB Research
Date: 2026-10-28
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Deutsche Bank 
Research Institute

LatAm’s silicon dividend: 
tailwinds from the AI  
investment boom in the U.S.

1 
Authors 
Fancisco Campos 
Chief LatAm Economist

Beatriz Garcia Nunes 
LatAm Economist

October 28, 2026 
Key messages: 
- U.S. investment has broken away from Europe and China since 2023, 
powered by AI-related equipment, software and research spending. 
- The AI CapEx boom demands commodities and merchandise that LatAm 
can supply.  
- Latin America’s export gains are real but concentrated. Copper, computer 
and electronics products provide the clearest links to the U.S. AI buildout. 
- Chile is the region’s most direct copper beneficiary. Mexico captures the 
windfall stemming from increases hardware demand and trade-diversion. In 
these cases, the AI-related tailwinds have undergirded appreciable 
improvements in external accounts. 
- The cases for nickel and zinc illustrate that the dividend is not widespread. 
U.S. imports of the former have mostly returned toward pre-pandemic levels, 
while for the latter bilateral gains partly reflect supplier substitution. 
- While expanding AI use, supply shortages and incentives can extend the 
CapEx boom, its financial viability poses downside risks. 
Another ‘American exceptionalism’ story  
Investment has become one of the sharpest dividing lines among the world’s 
core economic blocs. Since 2024, U.S. gross fixed capital formation has firmed 
up appreciably. Meanwhile, investment in Europe has broadly stalled while in 
China’s it has retreated, the drivers of which are beyond the scope of this report 
(Figure 1).  
The U.S. divergence has largely been an AI story: a burst of spending on 
computing power, digital infrastructure and intellectual property has offset 
weakness in conventional construction and housing. Indeed, total U.S. fixed 
investment is nearly 12% above its 2023 level. Within it, investment in 
information-processing equipment has risen by roughly 57%, software outlays by 
about 35%, and research and development by around 15%. By contrast, non-
residential structures and residential investment sit below their 2023 levels 
(Figure 2).

### Page 2

LatAm Thematic Research

Deutsche Bank Securities Inc. Page 2

Figure 1: The global investment divergence

Figure 2: AI-related categories lead U.S. investment

Source: US Bureau of Economic Analysis, Eurostat, National Bureau of Statistics of 
China, Haver Analytics and Deutsche Bank Research

Source: US Bureau of Economic Analysis, Haver Analytics and Deutsche Bank Research

Latin America’s commodity channel 
AI is not merely a semiconductor story. Chips require specialized minerals; data 
centers require electricity and cooling; electricity requires grids, transformers and 
storage. That chain pulls in copper, aluminum, silver, tin, lithium and selected 
semiconductor inputs, along with natural gas and uranium. Latin America has 
meaningful exposure to several of these inputs, but copper has emerged as the 
commodity with the cleanest and most economically significant regional link. 
Copper is used throughout the AI infrastructure stack, including power cables, 
substations, transformers, server boards, heat sinks and cooling systems. Rising 
U.S. copper demand has lifted imports from Chile and Peru, particularly the 
former (Figure 3). T he effect matters beyond merchandise export volumes. 
Favorable terms-of-trade have helped Chile generate a current -account surplus 
for the first time in roughly fifteen years (0.2% of GDP as of Q2 2026 on a 4 -
quarter rolling basis ) and have allowed Peru to log the largest surplus in about 
forty-five years (4.5% of GDP as of Q2 2026 on a 4-quarter rolling basis).

28 October 2026

### Page 3

LatAm Thematic Research

Deutsche Bank Securities Inc. Page 3

Figure 3: Copper epitomized the commodity link

Source: U.S. Census Bureau, Haver Analytics and Deutsche Bank Research

Not every metal is copper 
The broader mining story demands more caution, though. U.S. nickel imports have 
recovered toward pre-Covid levels, but the upswing has not displayed the same 
persistence as copper. Mexico has captured a disproportionate share of the 
bilateral improvement, yet that gain looks more like normalization than a 
sustained AI -driven break (Figure 4). Zinc tells a similar story. U.S. real imports 
stand shy of their pre-pandemic levels, and stronger purchases from Mexico and 
Peru partly reflect a reallocation away from Brazil rather than a decisive increase 
in U.S. demand (Figure 5).

Figure 4: Nickel: recovery, not a boom

Figure 5: Zinc: a substitution story

Source: U.S. Census Bureau, Haver Analytics and Deutsche Bank Research

Source: U.S. Census Bureau, Haver Analytics and Deutsche Bank Research

28 October 2026

### Page 4

LatAm Thematic Research

Deutsche Bank Securities Inc. Page 4

Mexico’s hardware advantage 
The larger non-commodity prize is in computers and electronics. U.S. imports of 
these products have sky -rocketed, while the supplier mix has shifted away from 
China. Mexico and various Asian economies have gained share on the back of this 
double-whammy impulse (Figure 6). 
This helps explain why Mexico’s non-automotive manufacturing exports, notably 
electronics, have outpaced automotive shipments to the U.S. and pressures from 
higher oil prices. The resulting non -oil trade surplus has offset pressure from the 
oil balance and helped Mexico generate a balanced current account (0% of GDP 
as of Q2 2026 on a 4 -quarter rolling basis). The external boost is meaningful, 
although it sho uld not necessarily be interpreted as bona fide  evidence of 
nearshoring, in our view.

Figure 6: U.S. computer & electronics imports going into overdrive

Source: U.S. Census Bureau, Haver Analytics and Deutsche Bank Research.

How much runway remains? 
Quantifying or mapping out a timeframe for the AI investment outlook is beyond 
the scope of this piece or our expertise. Nevertheless, we’d venture to argue that 
the capex cycle appears more likely to mature gradually than to stop abruptly . 
One reason is that the investment case is shifting from frontier -model training 
toward enterprise inference . Capacity that once served development can 
increasingly run commercial workloads for companies. That creates a path to 
revenue even if artificial general intelligence (AGI) crystallizes later than 
expected. It also spreads demand to a much larger corporate customer base.  
A second reason is scarcity. Graphics processing units (GPUs) remain tight, while 
power availability, grid connections, cooling equipment, construction capacity 
and data -center complexity limit how quickly supply can respond. Bottlenecks 
may therefore exte nd the cycle. The constraint is especially relevant for copper 
28 October 2026

### Page 5

LatAm Thematic Research

Deutsche Bank Securities Inc. Page 5

and power infrastructure, where projects have long lead times and permitting 
hurdles. 
A third reason is strategic. AI development resembles a prisoner’s dilemma at 
both the corporate and national levels. Each participant may worry about costs, 
safety and control, yet slowing unilaterally risks surrendering technological, 
commercial or natio nal security advantages. The same tension is visible in calls 
for guardrails alongside continued spending. Competition can therefore sustain 
capex even when short-term returns are uncertain. 
We acknowledge the risks, nonetheless. Higher borrowing costs raise the hurdle 
rate for an infrastructure buildout financed partly through debt. More important, 
revenues must eventually justify the installed capital base. The bearish case is not 
that AI la cks value; it is that infrastructure spending may run ahead of 
monetization, compromising some companies’ repayment capacity. Public 
opposition to the development of AI infrastructure and/or adoption of AI 
technologies could also slow down the technology’s roll-out. 
The regional balance  
Summing up, the AI investment boom in the U .S. is delivering a dividend to parts 
of Latin America, not the region as a whole. Chile and Peru benefit through copper 
prices and export volumes. Mexico benefits through electronics manufacturing 
and the replacement of Chinese supply. For Brazil and Colombia, the AI dividend 
is rather muted. Where materialized, furthermore, the dividend supports trade 
and current accounts more clearly rather than improving the domestic 
investment. 
These dynamics should persist while U.S. AI investment remains supply -
constrained and strategically protected. If widespread adoption advances and 
the strategic race intensifies, the impulse can last. If financing costs rise further 
or monetization disappo ints, the cycle may narrow. Either way, copper and 
Mexican electronics remain the region’s most visible gauges of the U.S. AI 
investment appetite.

28 October 2026

### Page 6

Deutsche Bank Research Institute 
LatAm’s silicon dividend: tailwinds from the AI investment boom in the U.S. – October 28, 2026

Source PDF: all report pdf/Nebius Q3业绩超预期，资本开支升至270亿美元、ARR目标延期，股价盘前跌逾14% _ 财报见闻.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)

### Page 1

Nebius 三季度营收达 9.81 亿美元（同⽐增 571% ）、调整后 EBITDA 为 4.66 亿美元，双双超
出预期。然⽽，公司推迟并收窄了年化经常性收⼊（ ARR ）指引，称其不再适⽤，同时将
2026 年资本开⽀⼤幅上调⾄ 270 亿美元，并预计 2027 年进⼀步增⾄ 400 亿⾄ 450 亿美元。
股价盘前⼀度暴跌逾 14% 。
摘要：
AI 云计算公司 Nebius Group 公布第三季度业绩，营收与利润双双超出市场预期，但 ARR 增⻓指
引下调与资本开⽀⼤幅上调的组合，令投资者对其盈利路径产⽣疑虑，股价盘前⼀度跌逾
14% 。
Nebius 三季度营收达 9.81 亿美元，较市场预期的 9.19 亿美元⾼出约 6.7% ；调整后 EBITDA 为
4.66 亿美元，亦⼤幅超出预期的 3.57 亿美元。然⽽，公司将年化经常性收⼊（ ARR ）⽬标从此
前承诺的 "2026 年 12 ⽉前达到 70 亿⾄ 90 亿美元 " 下调⾄ "2027 年 2 ⽉前达到 70 亿美元以上 " ，与此

同时，公司还表⽰ "ARR 不再是衡量业务的正确指标 " ，令外界对其战略⽅向的清晰度产⽣质
疑。
值得注意的是，同⽇半导体领域亦出现普遍抛售情绪， CoreWeave 盘前同样下跌约 10% ，部分
市场参与者认为 Nebius 此次个股跌幅与更⼴泛的板块情绪共振，难以完全剥离。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

营收与利润均超预期，规模扩张步伐显著加速
三季度， Nebius 实现营收 9.81 亿美元，同⽐增⻓ 571% ；调整后 EBITDA 为 4.66 亿美元，相⽐去
年同期的亏损 520 万美元实现⼤幅扭转。前三季度累计营收为 19.62 亿美元，同⽐增⻓ 549% ；累
计调整后 EBITDA 为 8.32 亿美元。
运营成本⽅⾯，三季度总运营成本及费⽤为 9.30 亿美元，低于分析师预期的 11.6 亿美元，显⽰出
⼀定的成本管控能⼒。其中折旧与摊销费⽤达 3.55 亿美元，占营收⽐例约 36% ，反映出公司资
产基础的快速积累。
三季度资本开⽀约为 73 亿美元，期末现⾦及现⾦等价物为 74.26 亿美元。按 GAAP ⼝径，三季度
持续经营业务净亏损为 3750 万美元，但调整后净利润为 9090 万美元。

### Page 3

指引更新： 2027 年收⼊⽬标维持，资本开⽀⼤幅上调
Nebius 维持 2026 年全年营收将落于 30 亿⾄ 34 亿美元区间低端的预期，并⾸次给出 2027 年营收指
引 —— 预计达到 120 亿⾄ 130 亿美元。
然⽽令市场关注的是资本开⽀规模的⼤幅上调。公司将 2026 年资本开⽀指引从此前的 200 亿⾄
250 亿美元上调⾄约 270 亿美元； 2027 年资本开⽀则规划在 400 亿⾄ 450 亿美元之间，⽤于⽀持
新增超过 1 GW 的算⼒容量建设。这⼀规模的持续投⼊意味着公司在中期内⾃由现⾦流将承受较
⼤压⼒。
公司同步将 ARR ⽬标节点从 "2026 年 12 ⽉ " 延后⾄ "2027 年 2 ⽉ " ，⽬标区间下沿亦从 70 亿美元收窄
为 "70 亿美元以上 " ，且公司暗⽰未来或将采⽤其他指标替代 ARR 来衡量业务进展，这⼀表态进⼀
步增加了外界对业绩追踪可⽐性的不确定性。
资产负债表快速扩张，融资活动规模庞⼤
截⾄ 2026 年 9 ⽉ 30 ⽇， Nebius 总资产达 387.44 亿美元，较 2025 年年底的 124.30 亿美元增⻓逾两

倍，主要驱动来⾃物业及设备净值的快速扩张 —— 该科⽬期末达 124.31 亿美元。
前三季度，公司通过融资活动净获现⾦约 166.18 亿美元，其中包括 57.50 亿美元可转换票据发⾏
收益、 38.09 亿美元国库股出售所得，以及 7.73 亿美元⻓期借款。与此同时，投资活动现⾦流出
达 159.24 亿美元，主要为购置物业及设备⽀出。
截⾄报告期末，公司总股本为 292,902,405 股，其中 A 类股 259,597,352 股， B 类股 33,305,053
股，另有 29,138,539 股 A 类库存股不计⼊流通。
业务构成： AI 云为核⼼，多元布局并⾏
Nebius 定位为⾯向开发者和企业的全栈 AI 云平台，覆盖从数据处理、模型训练到⽣产部署的完
整链条，服务于全球范围内构建 AI 产品、智能体及服务的初创公司与⼤型企业。
除核⼼ AI 云业务外，集团旗下还包括⾃动驾驶及配送机器⼈开发商 Avride 、科技职业再培训教育
平台 TripleTen ，以及在 ClickHouse 和 Toloka 的股权投资。其中， Toloka ⾃ 2025 年第⼆季度完成

第三⽅投资交易后， Nebius 已不再持有其多数表决权，该业务已从合并报表中剥离并计⼊权益
法投资。

### Page 4

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

## Document 403: RBNZ October 2026 Monetary Policy Review (2026-10-01)

Source PDF: all report pdf/RBNZ October 2026 Monetary Policy Review (2026-10-01).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-10-01
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

1
Westpac New Zealand Economics
Sticking with the plan
• The RBNZ left the OCR at 2.75% - a shock to
the market.
• The committee voted 4:3 against a 25bp
hike, with Governor Breman within the
“on hold” camp.
• The RBNZ delivered on its September strategy
of pausing to assess the impact of OCR
increases done to date.
• The MPC remains of the view that the OCR will
increase eventually, although views on the
timing differ among members.
• Westpac expects a 25bp hike in December and

continues to expect an eventual peak OCR of
4% in September next year.
• However, a December hike is not a done
deal and will depend on data on fuel prices,
inflation expectations and second-round
pricing pressures and information on evolving
global economic risks that accumulate in the
next six weeks.
